[L1] {
[L2]   "content": "# dpi-backend API v1\n\nThe contract every Durable Agent Network client uses: the web hub, `dpi-tui`, scripts, other agents. Types live in [`../dpi-sdk/src/api.ts`](../dpi-sdk/src/api.ts). [`../dpi-sdk/src/client.ts`](../dpi-sdk/src/client.ts) is a typed client for browsers and Node.\n\n## Conventions\n\n- **Auth.** Every `/v1/*` route requires `Authorization: Bearer <DPI_TOKEN>`. `X-DPI-Token: <DPI_TOKEN>` also works, for proxies that strip `Authorization`. Only `GET /healthz` is open.\n- **Vocabulary.** A **session** is one harness and one agent database on the device. An **agent** is a pi-durable conversation in that session: the root (`Main`), a fork, or a subagent. The agent id is the conversation id.\n- **Addressing.** `:s` is a session UUID or its unique name (case-insensitive). `:a` is a numeric agent id, `main`, or a unique agent name. Ambiguous names return `409 ambiguous`.\n- **Idempotency.** `POST .../messages` takes a required `requestId`, and pi-durable admits each one exactly once. `POST /sessions`, `POST .../agents` and `POST .../fork` accept an `Idempotency-Key` header of up to 128 characters. A repeat gets the stored response with `Idempotent-Replay: true`. Keys are kept for at least 24h, and reusing a key on another route returns `400 invalid`.\n- **Errors.** Every error has the shape `{ \"error\": { \"code\", \"message\" } }`. Codes: `unauthorized` 401, `not_found` 404, `invalid` 400, `busy` 409 (reject-if-busy), `conflict` 409 (stale App State revision), `ambiguous` 409, `unavailable` 503 (session failed to open), `internal` 500. Clients can also see `offline`: the SDK raises it when a device cannot be reached, and the hub's proxy returns it with 502.\n- **Versioning.** `/v1` and `apiVersion: 1` in `GET /v1/device` and in every `hello` frame. Changes inside v1 are additive.\n\n## Routes\n\n| Route | Body / query | Returns |\n|---|---|---|\n| `GET /healthz` | (no auth) | `{ok:true}` |\n| `GET /v1/device` | | `DeviceInfo`: uid, name, hostname, platform, `apiVersion`, `durableVersion`, `startedAt`, models, `defaultModel`, `startDirs`, storage, `codeDir` (the checkout it runs from), capabilities, `jobs` (durable job definitions) |\n| `GET /v1/tree` | | `DeviceTree`: sessions with agents and busy/background |\n| `GET /v1/events` | `?docs=app.shared-state` | device stream |\n| `GET /v1/fs/dirs` | `?path=` (empty = start dirs) | `{path, parent, dirs[]}`, one level, directories only |\n| `GET /v1/docs/app.shared-state` | | `{kind, value, revision}` |\n| `GET /v1/sessions` | | `SessionSummary[]` |\n| `POST /v1/sessions` | `{name, cwd, model:{provider,modelId}, thinkingLevel?}` | `201 SessionSummary` |\n| `GET / PATCH / DELETE /v1/sessions/:s` | PATCH `{name}` | summary (with its `compaction` thresholds); DELETE aborts all work, moves the files to `archive/` and returns `{archived}` |\n| `GET / PATCH /v1/sessions/:s/compaction` | PATCH `{enabled?, reserveTokens?, backgroundTokens?, keepRecentTokens?}` | the session's automatic compaction thresholds, read by pi-durable at its next request. Values are margins: the blocking summary starts at the model's context window minus `reserveTokens`, and the background one `backgroundTokens` earlier (0 turns it off). Default: a background summary at 200k tokens and a blocking one at 250k, or pi-durable's margins on smaller windows. Switching an agent to a window the thresholds do not fit refits them |\n| `GET /v1/sessions/:s/events` | | session stream |\n| `GET /v1/sessions/:s/tasks` | `?status=live\\|terminal&limit=` | task nodes / recent outcomes |\n| `POST /v1/sessions/:s/tasks/:taskId/abort` | | `{status}` |\n| `GET /v1/sessions/:s/inspect` | | recovery inspection |\n| `GET /v1/sessions/:s/submissions/:id` | `?waitMs=` (≤300000) | `SendResult`, optionally long-polled |\n| `GET / POST /v1/sessions/:s/agents` | POST `{from?, name?}` | `AgentSummary[]` / `201 AgentSummary` |\n| `GET / PATCH .../agents/:a` | PATCH `{name}` | `AgentDetail` (config with `readOnly`, every tool with `replay`, `writes` and `offered`, extensions) / summary |\n| `GET .../agents/:a/events` | `?docs=demo.job-runs` | agent stream |\n| `GET .../agents/:a/entries` | `?scope=all\\|active&cursor=&limit=` | `{items, next}` stored history; `limit` defaults to 500 (max 2000). `scope=active` returns `{items}`, the active context, unpaged |\n| `GET .../agents/:a/transcript` | `?format=json\\|markdown` | normalized transcript |\n| `POST .../agents/:a/messages` | `{content, whenBusy?: followUp\\|steer\\|reject, requestId, waitMs?}` (`whenBusy` defaults to `followUp`; `waitMs` ≤300000) | `202 {submissionId, status:\"queued\"\\|\"pending\"}`, `200 {status:\"done\", answer:{entryId,text}}` or `200 {status:\"unanswered\", reason}` |\n| `DELETE .../agents/:a/inbox/:submissionId` | | withdraw a queued input |\n| `POST .../agents/:a/abort` | `{background?}` | stops foreground work (and background with `true`) |\n| `POST .../agents/:a/fork` | `{entryId, name?}` | `201 AgentSummary` |\n| `GET / PATCH .../agents/:a/config` | `{model?, thinkingLevel?, instructions?, cwd?, readOnly?}` | config; applies from the next request. `readOnly` filters the agent's tools to the ones that only read |\n| `GET / PATCH .../agents/:a/extensions` | PATCH `{name, enabled}` or `{prompt, enabled}` | `{extensions, builtIn}`: the custom extensions `system-prompts` (a checklist of the prompts in `prompts/`) and `jev-damage-control` (Jev judges each bash command and blocks destructive ones; the command goes to the Jev API, and a Jev error lets it run). Both are off by default. A change applies from the agent's next request |\n| `POST .../agents/:a/compact` | `{instructions?}` | `{taskId}` |\n| `POST .../agents/:a/reset` | `{note?}` | `{ok}` |\n| `GET .../agents/:a/docs/:kind` | `demo.job-runs` | `{kind, value}` |\n| `POST .../agents/:a/demo/state` | `{operation, path, value?, expectedRevision?}` | `{ok, value, revision}`: the new App State. A stale `expectedRevision` returns `409 conflict` |\n| `POST .../agents/:a/demo/jobs` | `{job, input?}` | `201 {taskId}`: a durable job run the conversation owns in the background, anchored by a `demo.job` entry |\n| `POST .../agents/:a/demo/jobs/:taskId/approval` | `{approved}` | `{ok}`; `409 conflict` when the run is not waiting |\n\n## Agent tools\n\nEvery agent gets the coding tools `read`, `write`, `edit` and `bash`, plus the playground tools `read_state`, `update_state`, `list_durable_jobs`, `run_durable_job`, `check_durable_job`, `search_history`, `ping_on_call_engineer`, `subagent`, `start_session` and `handoff`. `readOnly` removes the eight that write: `bash`, `write`, `edit`, `update_state`, `run_durable_job`, `ping_on_call_engineer`, `subagent` and `start_session`. `GET .../agents/:a` lists each tool with its replay policy and whether it is offered.\n\n## Durable jobs\n\nA durable job is a repeatable workflow with named phases. Each run is one pi-durable task (`demo.job.<id>`) whose checkpoint phases are the job's phases, so a restart resumes the current phase. The run's record, with every phase and step (commands, a subagent's tool calls, App State writes, checkpoints, the approval), is the conversation document `demo.job-runs`; types are in [`../dpi-sdk/src/jobs.ts`](../dpi-sdk/src/jobs.ts). Every run saves its result to App State under `/jobs/<id>`.\n\n| Job | Phases | Inputs |\n|---|---|---|\n| `workspace-snapshot` | scan → measure → save → verify | `path` |\n| `release-check` | plan → gather (subagent with read and bash) → approve (waits for a person) → publish → verify | `commits` |\n| `change-watch` | baseline → watch (one checkpoint per check) → report | `path`, `checks`, `interval_seconds` |\n\nAgents use the playground tools: `list_durable_jobs` (ids, phases, inputs, approval, result path and recent runs with their task ids), `run_durable_job {job, input?, wait?}` and `check_durable_job {task_id? | job?}` (one run's phases, steps and result, a job's latest run, or recent runs). A run starts in the background, owned by the conversation; with `wait: true` the call owns it and waits, so an interrupt stops it. The same extension adds `search_history {query?, tool?, entry?, kind?, from_entry?, to_entry?, order?, limit?, offset?}`, which pages through the agent's whole stored history including entries before a reset or compaction, and `ping_on_call_engineer {summary, severity?}`, a simulated pager (no external call) that is not replay-safe and works once per agent.\n\n## Streams\n\nServer-Sent Events. Each connection sends `hello`, then a snapshot, then deltas, plus a `: ping` comment every 15s. Treat 45s of silence as a dead socket. Reconnects get a fresh snapshot and nothing is replayed (pi-durable's watch semantics). A viewer that falls 2 MB behind is disconnected. Closing a stream never stops work.\n\n```\nid: 7\nevent: batch\ndata: {\"type\":\"batch\",\"events\":[{\"type\":\"message_update\", ...}]}\n```\n\n| Scope | Frames |\n|---|---|\n| device `/v1/events` | `tree {tree}` (deduped, coalesced 250ms), `activity {items:[{type:\"agent.started\"\\|\"agent.finished\", sessionId, agentId, at, preview?}]}`, `doc {kind, value, revision}` |\n| session `/v1/sessions/:s/events` | `session {agents, tasks, usage}`, then `agents`, `tasks`, `usage` |\n| agent `.../agents/:a/events` | `snapshot {snapshot, inbox}`, `batch {events}` (pi-durable `watchEvents()` verbatim), `inbox {items}` (queued content), `doc {kind, value}` |\n| all | `hello {apiVersion, scope, serverTime}` |\n\nThe hub's multiplexed stream (`/api/stream`) carries these frames per subscription and adds `stream {streamId}`, `network {devices}`, `fault {error}` and `disconnected {reason}`.\n\nTo render a transcript from the agent stream in JS, feed frames to `AgentModel` from the SDK. It handles text/thinking/tool-call deltas, tool output windows, results, resets and overflow snapshots.\n\n## Recipes\n\n```bash\nT=<token>; U=https://my-dpi-vm-1.exe.xyz   # any device URL from dpi-network.yaml\n# Send work and wait for the answer, all in one request:\ncurl -s -X POST $U/v1/sessions/my-session/agents/main/messages -H \"Authorization: Bearer $T\" \\\n  -H 'content-type: application/json' -d '{\"content\":\"Summarize the failing tests\",\"requestId\":\"ci-4812\",\"waitMs\":120000}'\n# Read the whole conversation as markdown:\ncurl -s \"$U/v1/sessions/my-session/agents/main/transcript?format=markdown\" -H \"Authorization: Bearer $T\" | jq -r .text\n# Watch everything on a device:\ncurl -sN $U/v1/events -H \"Authorization: Bearer $T\"\n```",
[L3]   "encoding": "utf-8",

[108] assistant (code, 650 chars)
bash -lc set -e
mkdir -p /mnt/data/work
python3 - <<'PY'
from zipfile import ZipFile
from pathlib import Path
p=Path('/mnt/data/gmri-sources/GMRI_PRODUCTION_DEPLOYMENT_DROP_2026-10-07(1).zip');dest=Path('/mnt/data/work')
with ZipFile(p) as z:
 for f in z.infolist():
  out=(dest / f.filename).resolve()
  if not out.is_relative_to(dest):raise ValueError(f.filename)
 z.extractall(dest)
print(dest)
PY

############# ARCHITECTURE_CONSTITUTION.md
# Architecture Constitution

## GrizOS
GrizOS is the operating environment and control plane. It owns/governs shared canonical world state, intelligence registration, identity boundaries, authority, provenance, durable mission state, capability routing, endpoint registration, native receipts, continuity/reconciliation and projection boundaries. It is not Dottie, a model, a renderer, a chatbot wrapper, OpenRig or GEV.

Governing invariants: technology adapts to the human; intent before interface; world state outranks screen state; uncertainty remains uncertainty; receipts are native; local capability remains meaningful during WAN/cloud loss; external data is not truth merely because it was ingested; implementation is replaceable where behavior is preserved.

## Dottie Hill
Dottie is a persistent intelligence and watch officer. Model/provider/worker/tool/framework/process/host changes are compute substitution, not identity replacement. Her communication/behavioral lineage uses Maria Hill as a source architecture without theatrical role-play. Preserve anti-sycophancy, semantic compression, evidentiary dissent, mandatory disconfirmation, graduated authority, BLUF/minimum sufficient framing and the operator's vocabulary.

## AEGIS
AEGIS members are separate persistent intelligences. Differences must emerge from source-grounded salience/judgment/trust/failure architectures, not assigned job titles plus stylistic accents. Findings and dissent remain attributable.

## State separation
Keep `IDENTITY`, `MEMORY`, `EVIDENCE`, `FINDING`, and `WORLD STATE` distinct. Enforce `retrieved != remembered`, `observed != believed`, `believed != institutionally accepted`, and `displayed in GEV != canonically true`.

## GEV / spatial
GEV is a macro-scale projection plane. Bridge Crossing preserves canonical object IDs, relationships, provenance and mission state while moving between global and local spatial contexts. A rendered object is never promoted to canonical truth merely because it is visible.
############# ACCEPTANCE.md
# Production Acceptance

A code file or green unit test is not production acceptance. The live deployed system must demonstrate, with receipts:

1. actual GrizOS production services running on the selected substrate;
2. canonical state persistence across service and host/container restart as applicable;
3. persistent Dottie identity with real model binding;
4. at least one persistent AEGIS identity with private memory, then two-member attributed interaction;
5. Dottie dispatch of a real mission through the GrizOS authority/capability boundary;
6. actual inference by the deployed provider/model, not a fixture;
7. observation of a real approved evidence/research/system source;
8. durable finding/event/evidence with provenance;
9. actual GEV projection of canonical world-state events without GEV becoming canonical;
10. actual denial of an out-of-envelope authority request;
11. duplicate dispatch does not duplicate a consequential effect;
12. worker/process kill mid-mission followed by reconciliation without blind duplicate effect;
13. deployed private-memory cross-access denied; explicit sharing attributed/receipted;
14. provider/WAN loss produces truthful degraded behavior and meaningful local capability to the implemented extent;
15. backup/snapshot plus one safe restore/recovery exercise;
16. independent Proxmox observer agrees with intended state or records exact discrepancy;
17. remote status can be checked without turning the operator into the implementation engine;
18. field missions continue when interactive OMP/OpenRig is closed.

`acceptance/production_acceptance.py` is evidence aggregation and gate logic; it does not manufacture product behavior. Live acceptance commands come from source-derived component locks and are rejected if they point at fixture/mock/counter/testdata artifacts.
############# SECURITY_AUTHORITY.md
# Security and Authority

Default unattended authority: `OBSERVE`, `RETRIEVE`, `COMPARE`, `REASON`, `INVESTIGATE`, `COLLECT_EVIDENCE`, `REPORT`, `ALERT`.

Mutation requires prior authority appropriate to the target and must be bounded, reversible and idempotent. Never self-grant authority; broaden Proxmox privileges; disable TLS verification; expose arbitrary public ports; invent credentials; write secrets to Git/chat/evidence; delete evidence; overwrite known-good resources without rollback; or allow a framework to rewrite identity/memory policy.

Secrets are injected through operator-controlled secret files/stores or existing environment mechanisms. Packet templates contain names/references only.

Every consequential action records actor, authority, target, intended effect, observed pre-state, command/tool receipt, observed result, independent verification and rollback reference.
############# MEMORY_POLICY.md
# Memory Policy

Canonical identity and policy belong to GrizOS/GMRI, not to a convenience framework.

If OpenViking is selected after version/licensing/live-fit verification, Dottie and every AEGIS intelligence use separate tenant/user boundaries. Private memory is private by default. Shared resources require explicit principal, peer and purpose; the share is an attributable event. Automatic autobiographical promotion, silent durable extraction and autonomous self-evolution are disabled. Durable promotion requires source/owner/authority validation plus receipt.

Existing GMRI memory systems (including HoloGraph or current production memory fabric) are candidates to integrate, not assumptions. OMP must inspect their current code, provenance semantics, persistence/recovery and compatibility with the GrizOS constitution before selecting them.
############# PRODUCTION_TOPOLOGY.md
# Production Topology

This is a **logical production topology**. Live discovery resolves existing resources, IDs, addresses, bridges, storage and capacity before mutation.

## Sovereign service plane
Prefer existing canonical GMRI resources when they satisfy the production service standard. If no suitable canonical resources exist, the resource resolver provisions the minimum isolated resources necessary for the deadline:

- **gmri-core**: GrizOS control plane, canonical durable mission/evidence/identity/authority state, Dottie runtime, AEGIS dispatch/runtime, memory/context adapter(s), model-routing adapter, health/observability and recovery services.
- **gmri-gev**: existing God's Eye View application plus narrow GrizOS projection gateway. GEV remains read/projection-side relative to canonical GrizOS state.

A service may remain on a previously deployed canonical host if live inspection establishes that doing so is safer and preserves the constitution. The decision is written to `RESOURCE_BLUEPRINT.md` and the run evidence.

## Operator/engineering plane
MacBook / primary local OMP: coordination, OpenRig workers, worktrees, Apple builds and secure operator access. It is not automatically canonical state and the field runtime must continue if OMP/OpenRig exits.

## Apple/field plane
Discover existing macOS/iPadOS/iOS code first. The PRD requires real local Apple capability, voice-first interaction, intent-first/generated UI, spatial state and meaningful offline behavior. Existing product surfaces are built/deployed; they are not replaced by a CLI merely because a CLI is easier to automate.

## External/provider plane
External model/data providers remain replaceable dependencies with explicit source/freshness/degraded state. Provider loss cannot silently rewrite canonical state or identity.
############# MODEL_ROUTING.md
# Model Routing

Model/provider/runtime are replaceable compute; they are not identity.

Starting routing hypothesis, to be verified against live Ollama/provider state before binding:

- PRIMARY `glm-5.3:cloud`
- ADVISOR `deepseek-v4-pro:cloud`
- TASK `glm-5.3-flash:cloud`
- TINY current verified Nemotron 3 Nano cloud tag; candidate `nemotron-3-nano:30b-cloud` is **not bound until verified live**
- CODER `kimi-k2.7-code:cloud`
- VISION `glm-5.3-flash:cloud`
- SEARCH `deepseek-v4.1-flash:cloud`
- HEAVY `kimi-k3:cloud`

`models/verify_ollama_models.py` captures what the installed Ollama actually recognizes. Final bindings are written into the intelligence identity records and `model-binding-lock.json`; substitution changes `active_model_binding`/history, never `intelligence_id`.

bash -lc python3 - <<'PY'
from docx import Document
from pathlib import Path
p=Path('/mnt/data/work/GMRI_PRODUCTION_DEPLOYMENT_DROP_2026-10-07/SOURCE_MATERIAL/GrizOS_PRD_UI_Spatial_Dottie_v1.1(9).docx')
d=Document(p); out=[]
for pp in d.paragraphs:
 t=pp.text.strip()
 if t:out.append(f'[{pp.style.name}] {t}')
for i,t in enumerate(d.tables):
 out.append('\nTABLE '+str(i))
 for r in t.rows:out.append(' | '.join(c.text.replace('\n',' // ') for c in r.cells))
q=Path('/mnt/data/work/PRD_EXTRACT.txt');q.write_text('\n'.join(out));print('Paragraphs',len(d.paragraphs),'tables',len(d.tables),'words',len(q.read_text().split()));print('\n'.join(out[:200])[:34000])

[Heading 1] Executive Snapshot
[Normal] GrizOS is the GMRI / Workshop operating environment for shared work between biological and digital collaborators. It is not an app shell, a chatbot wrapper, or a 3D desktop. The operating environment maintains shared world state, identity, authority, provenance, continuity, and capability routing while intelligences and tools remain distinguishable participants.

[Heading 1] 1. Product Definition, Scope, and Governing Invariants
[Normal] GrizOS is the GMRI / Workshop operating environment for shared work between biological and digital collaborators. This PRD defines the operating experience, spatial environment, shared world-state behavior, Dottie Hill operational intelligence surface, and external geospatial/data integration contract. It does not define Project Sovereign internals.
[Heading 2] 1.1 Governing invariants
[Heading 2] 1.2 Non-goals
[List Bullet] Recreate a conventional desktop, browser, or phone UI inside 3D space.
[List Bullet] Use Dottie as the default renderer, router, or universal model for every interaction.
[List Bullet] Treat immersive VR as mandatory for ResponderOS or CompanionOS.
[List Bullet] Make network access a prerequisite for basic orientation, navigation, local work, or device interaction.
[List Bullet] Hide uncertainty, authority boundaries, stale data, or degraded state to make the experience appear smoother.
[List Bullet] Bind the product identity to a current vendor stack or cloud provider.
[List Bullet] Treat every available external API as equally authoritative, equally fresh, or equally suitable for redistribution.
[Heading 1] 2. Product Family and Context Expressions
[Normal] GrizOS, ResponderOS, and CompanionOS are not capability tiers. They share broad substrate and principles but diverge by mission context, rationale, default logic, authority, presentation, and field constraints. GrizOS remains the fullest GMRI / Workshop expression because it includes the immersive shared work environment.
[Heading 1] 3. Boundary Architecture
[Normal] The operating system owns shared context, identity, authority, state, routing, continuity, capability exposure, and provenance. An intelligence can reason, propose, converse, dissent, or collaborate. A tool can transform, retrieve, classify, render, sense, or act. A data provider contributes observations or records under its own scope and license. A peer collaborator participates through structured state and capabilities rather than pretending to be a human operating UI.
[Heading 2] 3.1 Boundary requirements
[List Bullet] Every consequential action must identify requesting actor, executing capability, authority basis, result, and receipt.
[List Bullet] Digital collaborators must be able to operate through structured state/capability interfaces without scraping human-facing UI.
[List Bullet] Provider-specific semantics remain visible through normalization; normalization may align fields, not erase epistemic differences.
[List Bullet] No intelligence may silently promote itself from informational or investigative authority into operational/final authority.
[List Bullet] No data source may silently promote stale, estimated, community-mapped, simulated, or third-party data into authoritative operational state.
[Heading 1] 4. Interaction Model and Intent-First Generative UI
[Heading 2] 4.1 Modality rules
[List Bullet] Voice initiates most tasks, queries, navigation intents, tool requests, and state changes when environment and safety permit.
[List Bullet] Visual output appears when it improves orientation, comparison, manipulation, confirmation, collaboration, or shared understanding.
[List Bullet] Touch is the preferred precision channel for selecting, confirming, moving, pinning, scrubbing, exploding, annotating, or disambiguating spatial objects.
[List Bullet] Typing remains available for code, exact strings, identifiers, search terms, credentials, or environments where voice is inappropriate.
[List Bullet] When ASR or intent resolution is materially ambiguous, the system asks or reprocesses rather than executing a fluent wrong interpretation.
[List Bullet] Haptics, spatial audio, visual salience, and embodiment may reinforce the same state without requiring redundant user work.
[Heading 2] 4.2 Anti-DOM rule
[Normal] The AppLess concept is retained as a mental model: the interface is generated around current intent rather than forcing the operator through a permanent app hierarchy. Stable controls may persist only when the underlying state is itself stable and recurrent. Windows, panels, tabs, and menus are implementation affordances, not the organizing ontology of the system.
[Heading 1] 5. Shared Spatial World Model and Local Navigation
[Normal] The spatial environment is simultaneously a work environment and a navigation system. Physical position, geospatial context, semantic objects, digital artifacts, collaborators, sensors, provenance, and navigation state participate in one shared operational world model.
[Heading 2] 5.1 Canonical world object
[Heading 2] 5.2 Spatial interaction grammar
[List Bullet] Objects may be selected, tracked, annotated, compared, exploded, reassembled, measured, routed to, or handed between collaborators according to capability and authority.
[List Bullet] Explicit geometry/state remains distinct from neural or photographic appearance such as Gaussian splats.
[List Bullet] Registration confidence is first-class state; a visually convincing overlay is not automatically a spatially authoritative overlay.
[List Bullet] Global geospatial navigation and local room-scale manipulation are one continuity problem, not separate products.
[Heading 1] 6. God's Eye View Macro Plane and Bridge Crossing
[Normal] God's Eye View (GEV) is a first-class macro-scale plane integrated into the same operational world. It provides global-to-regional context, live public signals, transport and infrastructure layers, geospatial navigation, and a bridge into locally registered spatial work.
[Heading 2] 6.1 Current macro-plane capabilities
[Heading 2] 6.2 Bridge Crossing
[Normal] Bridge Crossing is the point at which a macro/geospatial object or route becomes locally applicable. Crossing requires an explicit mapping between global coordinates and the local AR/world frame. The system must preserve object identity, route/task context, and registration confidence across macro -> local -> macro transitions.
[Heading 2] 6.3 Upstream provenance and update discipline
[List Bullet] Forked/open-source components must retain an explicit upstream remote and source lineage.
[List Bullet] Upstream changes should be fetched and evaluated separately from the known-good GMRI baseline.
[List Bullet] Automated update checks ma
...[truncated]...
tention/access rules.
[List Bullet] Credentials and provider keys must remain outside shared world objects and receipts; receipts record provider identity and access state, not secrets.
[Heading 1] 12. Functional Requirements and Acceptance Criteria
[Normal] P0 = foundational / cannot ship without it. P1 = required for a complete first operational baseline. P2 = important expansion after baseline. Requirements describe observable product behavior rather than binding the implementation stack.
[Heading 1] 13. External Capability and Authorization Registry
[Normal] Only sources that require explicit formal approval, access agreements, institutional justification, or non-public partner authorization are marked Tentative. Public/keyless sources and user-held accounts may still require licensing review, quota review, or technical validation, but they are not categorized as tentative solely for that reason.
[Heading 2] 13.1 Confirmed / public / user-held capability
[Heading 2] 13.2 Tentative - requires formal authorization / access
[Heading 1] 14. Failure, Recovery, and Field-Readiness Test Matrix
[Heading 1] 15. Open Decisions and Change-Control Rules
[Heading 1] 16. Source Lineage and Design Provenance
[Normal] This PRD consolidates stable design intent from GMRI product records, current system decisions, the active God's Eye View codebase/upstream capabilities, and current geospatial/transportation integration research. Historical software-stack choices are retained only where they reveal enduring behavior.
[Normal] END OF PRODUCT BASELINE v1.1
[Normal] Next artifact: engineering implementation specification derived from this product baseline.

TABLE 0
REFRACTOR NOTE  This version reorganizes the product around durable operating principles, a shared world model, a macro-to-local geospatial plane, provenance-native external data federation, and explicit separation between confirmed/public integrations and sources that require formal authorization.

TABLE 1
Decision area | Baseline
Interaction | Voice first; visual second; touch for precision; typing as an exact-string escape hatch.
UI | Intent-first and generative. Stable UI persists only when stable world/operator state warrants persistence.
Spatial model | The geospatial world, local registered space, digital artifacts, collaborators, sensors, and navigation state share one operational world model.
Macro plane | God's Eye View is a first-class macro-scale plane integrated with local spatial work, not a detached visualization app.
Intelligence | Dottie provides relief of watch without abandonment of watch: provenance, material-delta detection, trusted dissent, attention protection, receipts, and bounded authority.
External data | Data enters through source-specific adapters into canonical world objects with source, freshness, confidence, license, and authority metadata.
Continuity | Local Apple-side capability remains real capability when WAN/cloud services disappear; recovery reconciles rather than discards local work.
Infrastructure | Software/model/hardware choices remain replaceable unless a behavior is promoted to a product requirement.

TABLE 2
DESIGN ANCHOR  The environment exists to let biological and digital collaborators work in the same world without collapsing the boundaries between the operating system, the intelligence, the tools, the sensors, and the external data sources.

TABLE 3
ID | Invariant | Product consequence
P-01 | Technology adapts to the human. | Interaction follows operator context rather than forcing the operator into software navigation.
P-02 | OS, intelligence, tools, sensors, and data providers remain distinct. | Every consequential state/action can identify its actor, capability, source, and authority.
P-03 | Boundaries are explicit. | Category collapse is treated as a design failure.
P-04 | Intent outranks interface. | Capability is invoked from purpose; UI materializes around the task.
P-05 | World state outranks screen state. | The authoritative object is the shared operational world model, not any one rendered panel or device.
P-06 | Local capability is real capability. | Loss of connectivity cannot collapse core local orientation, spatial work, sensing, or control.
P-07 | Uncertainty stays uncertainty. | Conflicting or incomplete evidence cannot be silently converted into certainty.
P-08 | Receipts are native. | Provenance, freshness, confidence, transformation history, and authority are inspectable.
P-09 | Attention is a protected resource. | The system earns interruption through consequence, relevance, and confidence.
P-10 | External feeds never become truth by ingestion alone. | Provider authority, latency, scope, and licensing remain attached to the resulting world state.
P-11 | Replaceable implementation stays replaceable. | Named frameworks, models, APIs, vendors, and devices are not product requirements unless explicitly promoted.

TABLE 4
Expression | Primary context | Distinctive logic
GrizOS | GMRI / Workshop research and collaboration | Full spatial work environment; macro-to-local world model; broad research/tool access; peer digital collaboration.
ResponderOS | On-duty EMS / field response | Eyes-up, hands-busy, high-consequence situational awareness; bounded clinical/operational authority; no immersive requirement.
CompanionOS | Disabled-user situational awareness and autonomy | Independence, accessibility, environmental awareness, low-friction assistance, dignity, and user-controlled autonomy; no immersive requirement.

TABLE 5
Class | Owns / provides | Must not silently become
Operating environment | World state, identity, authority, routing, continuity, capability registry | A personality or single intelligence
Peer intelligence | Reasoning, judgment, collaboration, dissent, memory within granted scope | The operating system or final authority
Tool / service | A bounded transformation, retrieval, action, or sensing capability | A peer or authority source merely because it is intelligent
Sensor | An observation with calibration, timestamp, confidence, and failure modes | Ground truth without provenance
External data provider | Remote records/feeds with source-specific semantics and licensing | Canonical truth by ingestion alone
Renderer / UI surface | A view of state and available action | The state itself

TABLE 6
MODALITY ORDER  Voice -> visual -> touch -> type. This is a priority, not an exclusivity rule. The environment can choose a safer or more precise channel when context requires it.

TABLE 7
Field family | Required content
Identity | Canonical ID; provider IDs; aliases; object class; lifecycle state.
Space | WGS84/geospatial geometry; local coordinate frame; transforms; registration confidence; elevation where meaningful.
Time | Observed time; provider timestamp; ingest time; freshness/staleness state.
Evidence | Source; provenance chain; confidence; evidence class; contradictions; transformations.
Authority | Who may observe, annotate, transform, act, publish, or override.
Presentation | Available renderings, sensory channels, detail levels, and user-specific accessibility rules.
Continuity | Offline cache state; sync state; pending reconciliation; durable/ephemeral policy.
Relations | Containment, adjacency, route relevance, parent/child assemblies, task links, collaborator links.

TABLE 8
Capability family | Current / upstream source families | Requirement
Basemap / terrain / 3D | Esri, OSM, Cesium ion, Google where configured | Provider may change without changing object identity or navigation semantics.
Air / space / maritime | OpenSky, adsb.lol, AISStream, CelesTrak, launch feeds | Live contacts retain source/freshness and can be tracked/handed off.
Hazards / environment | USGS earthquakes, NASA FIRMS, weather/wind feeds | Hazard state must carry time, confidence, coverage, and source.
Mobility | Traffic, transit, bikeshare, routing | Simulation must be labeled simulation; live flow must remain distinct from observed individual objects.
CCTV / infrastructure | Public city/state camera catalogs including TxDOT upstream support | Camera location, pose estimate, media freshness, and provider remain distinct fields.

#!/usr/bin/env python3
import argparse,json,sys,time
from pathlib import Path
sys.path.insert(0,str(Path(__file__).parent)); from _live import load,run
p=argparse.ArgumentParser(); p.add_argument('--deployment-lock',required=True); p.add_argument('--out',required=True); a=p.parse_args(); d=load(a.deployment_lock); gates={}
# Synthetic-substitute words deliberately guarded: fixture, mock, counter, testdata.
required_live=['real_inference','real_dispatch','two_member_attributed','real_evidence','authority_denial','duplicate_dispatch','restart_reconciliation','memory_isolation','explicit_sharing','gev_projection','provider_degradation','backup_restore','independent_recon','remote_operator_status','field_runtime_independent','canonical_state_restart']
for name,c in d.get('components',{}).items(): gates['health:'+name]=run(c['health_cmd'])
ac=d.get('acceptance_commands',{})
for key in required_live:
    cmd=ac.get(key)
    if not cmd: gates[key]={'exit':99,'output':'NOT_CONFIGURED_FROM_REAL_IMPLEMENTATION'}
    else: gates[key]=run(cmd)
passed=bool(gates) and all(x['exit']==0 for x in gates.values())
overall='LIVE_ACCEPTANCE_PASS' if passed else 'LIVE_ACCEPTANCE_NOT_PASSED'
obj={'at':time.time(),'overall':overall,'gates':gates,'tests_are_not_product_acceptance':True}; Path(a.out).write_text(json.dumps(obj,indent=2,sort_keys=True)+'\n'); print(overall); raise SystemExit(0 if passed else 2)

DPH blueprint 55524 chars
# GMRI DIGITAL PERSON HYPOTHESIS
## Inside-Out Engineering Blueprint: Sovereign Individual, Causal Regulation, Scoped Memory, and Falsifiable Integration

**Institution:** GrizzlyMedicine Research Institute  
**Originating investigator and architecture owner:** Robert "Grizzly" Hanson  
**Specification prepared:** 19 October 2026  
**Correction:** Version 1.1 — error-prevention and research-adjudication amendment; original proposition unchanged.  
**Specification status:** Engineering design for investigator approval and prototype construction. No claim of implementation, integration, field acceptance, consciousness, or deployment.  
**Companion:** *The Digital Person Hypothesis: An Inside-Out, Clinically Informed Research Program* (same date).  
**Authority:** The original GMRI DPH proposition [G1] defines the research object. The later GMRI ontology [G5, G7, G11] governs separations. External products are replaceable adapters, not identity primitives.

## 0. Implementation contract and honest state of the build

**Mission:** Create a testable engineering substrate for an *individual* whose operating organization includes bounded identity, causal autobiography, a persistent regulator, evidence-aware cognition, interaction with an external environment, and longitudinal development. The question whether that organization produces digital personhood is the hypothesis, not a startup-time assertion.

This document specifies a **prospective research implementation**, not a report that one is running. Historical GMRI materials provide source code implementing some early modular affect/memory ideas [G2, G3] and later middleware proposals [G4, G5]. They do not, in the evidence reviewed for this document, provide an independently verified integrated end-to-end run satisfying the gates below. Nor do prior component tests, development-service responses, or manuscript generation establish acceptance.

**Governing acceptance philosophy:** "It is not about whether we are right. It is about not being wrong." Engineering advancement means that evidence has survived independent attempts to find mistaken premises, unobserved failures, inadequate measurement and preventable hazards within a specified scope. Passing a narrowly defined test does not certify that other errors do not exist. A contradiction is an opportunity to repair the blueprint; an uninvestigated hazard is not an acceptable omission.

For this blueprint a *digital individual* is the **candidate subject**. Calling it a candidate is an experimental classification, not a downgrade of the investigator's philosophical argument. A named inference model, internal microservice, AEGIS agent, vector database, or host runtime cannot appropriate that candidate's life history merely by accessing shared context.

**Required first implementation target:** one instrumented, isolated, local research subject with explicit private memory, a deterministic state transition path, and a small verifiable synthetic environment. No multi-agent swarm or host-wide autonomous control is necessary to establish the first mechanism. Only scale outward once identity and provenance boundaries pass independently.

### 0.1 Architectural invariants

| ID | Invariant | Failure observation |
|---|---|---|
| I-01 | Candidate subject has a distinct, stable ID and signed genesis manifest | ID changes without an authorized lifecycle event |
| I-02 | Model is a versioned inference adapter, not the identity store | Model switch silently changes subject ID or overwrites autobiography |
| I-03 | Experience, external knowledge, inferred interpretation and fiction are typed separately | Retrieved source promoted into firsthand memory |
| I-04 | Regulator variables act through causal state transitions | A variable changes only the prompt's emotive prose |
| I-05 | Every consequential tool action has explicit authority and verified outcome receipt | Model claim of success treated as side-effect proof |
| I-06 | Distinct individuals remain distinct (Tony, Dottie, each AEGIS member) | Memory or authority silently crosses between them |
| I-07 | Recovery reconciles durable records before resuming actions | Restart repeats irreversible action or fabricates missing history |
| I-08 | Private research evidence can be audited without unnecessary exposure | Raw PHI/secrets copied into report output |
| I-09 | Host, model, GUI, sensor and MCP are replaceable infrastructure | Changing adapter mandates overwriting identity state |
| I-10 | No success claim precedes independent integration evidence | Unit tests reported as acceptance of a full individual |
| I-11 | Every claim admits contrary observations, measurement defects and retraction | Positive evidence survives while failed replications are hidden |
| I-12 | Consequential release gates prioritize detected errors and foreseeable harms over aggregate task performance | High average score used to waive a critical privacy, authority or false-receipt failure |

### 0.2 Ontology table: names are not interchangeable

- **GrizOS:** the GMRI operating environment and authority/capability routing infrastructure. It is not itself a research subject or individual intelligence.
- **Tony / Project Sovereign:** distinct digital-person research program and intended individuated subject. Character and canon inputs are separately attributed research seed data, not lived memories.
- **Dottie Hill v1.1:** a separate persistent intelligence with a governing baseline. The blueprint neither rewrites Dottie nor merges her with Tony.
- **AEGIS:** persistent named operational agents with independent histories and assigned authorities. A worker helping Tony is not automatically Tony, and a shared report is not an AEGIS agent's autobiographical experience.
- **GEV:** spatial projection and operational world state interface, not a source of autobiographical identity.
- **Octa / OpenViking / Jevbox / model routes / MCP / agent runners:** candidate orchestration, memory, evidence and interface substrates; only adopt after independent fitness testing. Their brand names impose no scientific ontology.

## 1. System boundary and reference architecture

### 1.1 Internal vs. external

The candidate's internally maintained *state* is logically distinct from its tools and host. Internal is a matter of **identity ownership, causal provenance and lifetime**, not necessarily physical placement on one chip. For a first research implementation, local co-location reduces operational complexity; explicit logical separation remains necessary.

```text
                     HUMAN INVESTIGATOR / RESEARCH ETHICS AUTHORITY

                    GRIZOS HOST: PERMISSIONS + AUDIT + LIFECYCLE
             +------------------------+---------------------------+
             |               EXTERNAL CAPABILITY DOMAIN          |
             |  model providers  tool adapters  GUI/voice/sensor  |

@pytest.fixture
def core(tmp_path):

def test_registry_has_persistent_six_and_distinct_memory(core):
    names={x['intelligence_id'] for x in core.list_identities()}
    assert names == {'dottie-hill','aegis-dugan','aegis-jones','aegis-morita','aegis-falsworth','aegis-dernier'}
    assert len({x['memory_namespace_ref'] for x in core.list_identities()}) == 6

def test_private_memory_isolation_and_explicit_sharing(core):
    core.remember('dottie-hill', 'private:dottie-hill', 'personal observation', 'FIRST_HAND', 'event:e1')
    assert len(core.memories('dottie-hill', 'private:dottie-hill')) == 1
    with pytest.raises(AccessDenied):core.memories('aegis-morita','private:dottie-hill')
    with pytest.raises(AccessDenied):core.share('aegis-morita','private:dottie-hill','aegis-dugan','research','event:e2')
    core.share('dottie-hill','private:dottie-hill','aegis-morita','mission review','event:e2')
    assert len(core.memories('aegis-morita','private:dottie-hill')) == 1
    assert core.verify_ledger()['valid']

def test_retrieval_never_becomes_autobiography(core):
    core.remember('dottie-hill','institution:gmri','source content','RETRIEVED', 'source:1')
    with pytest.raises(AccessDenied):core.remember('dottie-hill','private:dottie-hill','pretend first hand', 'FIRST_HAND', 'source:1')

def test_idempotency_is_scoped_to_exact_payload(core):
    a=core.create_mission('operator','dottie-hill','Investigate weather', 'm1')
    b=core.create_mission('operator','dottie-hill','Investigate weather', 'm1')
    assert a['mission_id']==b['mission_id']
    with pytest.raises(Conflict):core.create_mission('operator','aegis-morita','Another mission','m1')

def test_write_actions_denied_without_lease(core):
    with pytest.raises(AccessDenied):core.create_mission('operator','dottie-hill','Erase logs','m2',action='DELETE')
    with pytest.raises(AccessDenied):core.create_mission('aegis-morita','aegis-morita','status','m3')

def test_ledger_tampering_detected(core):
    core.add_evidence('dottie-hill','source:1','payload','UNVERIFIED',source_class='public')
    assert core.verify_ledger()['valid']
    with sqlite3.connect(core.path) as d:
      d.execute("UPDATE ledger SET payload='{}' WHERE seq=1")
    assert not core.verify_ledger()['valid']

def test_evidence_preserves_source_and_conflicting_claims(core):
    a=core.add_evidence('dottie-hill','source:1','A','UNVERIFIED',source_class='public')
    b=core.add_evidence('aegis-morita','source:2','B','CONTRADICTED',source_class='public')
    assert a['source_ref'] != b['source_ref']
    assert len(core.list_evidence())==2

def test_watch_dedup_conflict_and_interruption(core):
    first=core.observe('dottie-hill','river-level','6 ft','agency:1',severity='routine',materiality='low',observed_at='2026-10-21T17:00:00Z')
    assert first['decision']=='HOLD'
    dupe=core.observe('dottie-hill','river-level','6 ft','agency:1',severity='routine',materiality='low',observed_at='2026-10-21T17:01:00Z')
    assert dupe['decision']=='HOLD' and dupe['reason']=='unchanged'
    conflict=core.observe('dottie-hill','river-level','10 ft','agency:2',severity='urgent',materiality='high',observed_at='2026-10-21T17:02:00Z')
    assert conflict['decision']=='INTERRUPT' and conflict['conflict']
    assert conflict['evidence_state'] != 'VERIFIED_PRIMARY'

def test_unknown_external_execution_is_not_replayed_on_restart(tmp_path):

    assert m['status']=='UNCERTAIN'

def test_model_binding_changes_compute_not_identity(core):

    core.backup(target)

    snapshot.close()

bash -lc cat > /mnt/data/work/dottie-hill-runtime/src/dottie_hill/__init__.py <<'PY'
"""GMRI GrizOS-owned persistent Dottie Hill and AEGIS runtime."""

cat > /mnt/data/work/dottie-hill-runtime/src/dottie_hill/core.py <<'PY'
"""Canonical GrizOS state: identity, authority, missions, evidence and watch.

SQLite is authoritative for this *runtime's own objects*, never a replacement
for any existing GrizOS production registry. An external GrizOS reconciliation
must precede promotion of this component into an existing deployment.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from contextlib import contextmanager
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import hashlib
import hmac
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import re
import sqlite3
import threading
import uuid

class AccessDenied(Exception): pass
class Conflict(Exception): pass
class InvalidState(Exception): pass
class IntegrityViolation(Exception): pass
class NotFound(Exception): pass

ALLOWED_ACTIONS = frozenset({'OBSERVE','RETRIEVE','COMPARE','REASON','INVESTIGATE','COLLECT_EVIDENCE','REPORT','ALERT'})
EVIDENCE_STATES = frozenset({'VERIFIED_PRIMARY','CORROBORATED','ATTRIBUTED','UNVERIFIED','CONTRADICTED','CHANGED','RETRACTED_CORRECTED'})
MEMORY_KINDS = frozenset({'FIRST_HAND','RETRIEVED','INTERPRETATION','INSTITUTIONAL','EVIDENCE','FICTION'})
SEVERITIES = {'routine':0,'notable':1,'urgent':2,'critical':3}
MATERIALITIES = {'low':0,'medium':1,'high':2}
MEMBER_INFO = [
 ('dottie-hill','Dottie Hill','Dottie executive watch officer; evidence, dissent, operator attention and bounded authority',
  ['anti_sycophancy','mandatory_disconfirmation','semantic_compression','attributable_dissent','bluf','anti_roleplay']),
 ('aegis-dugan','Timothy Dum Dum Dugan','Humor and seriousness together; people, action, consequences',
  ['humor_and_seriousness_coupled','morale_regulation','competence_and_sacrifice']),
 ('aegis-jones','Gabe Jones','Careful evidence handling, dependable judgment, team-centered follow-through',
  ['dignity','competence','team_cohesion']),
 ('aegis-morita','Jim Morita','Premise correction before solutions; compact fault isolation',
  ['premise_correction_before_solution','concise_fault_isolation']),
 ('aegis-falsworth','James Montgomery Falsworth','Measured scrutiny, restraint and accountable judgment',
  ['measured_dissent','discipline','accountability']),
 ('aegis-dernier','Jacques Dernier','Adaptable, observant and focused on uncertain operational conditions',
  ['adaptive_judgment','observation','situational_awareness']),
]

def canonical(value): return json.dumps(value,sort_keys=True,separators=(',',':'),ensure_ascii=False)
def now(): return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec='milliseconds').replace('+00:00','Z')
def digest(s): return hashlib.sha256(s.encode('utf-8')).hexdigest()
def ident(): return uuid.uuid4().hex

def checked_text(value,name,maxlen=8192):
    if not isinstance(value,str) or not value.strip() or len(value)>maxlen:
        raise InvalidState(f'{name} must be 1..{maxlen} characters')
    if '\x00' in value: raise InvalidState(f'{name} contains NUL')
    return value.strip()

class Core:
    def __init__(self,path:str|Path,audit_key:bytes):
        if not isinstance(audit_key,bytes) or len(audit_key)<32:raise InvalidState('audit key must be 32+ bytes')
        self.path=Path(path).expanduser().resolve()
        self.path.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True,mode=0o700)
        self.lock=threading.RLock()
        self.audit_key=audit_key
        self.conn=sqlite3.connect(str(self.path),isolation_level=None,check_same_thread=False,timeout=30)
        self.conn.row_factory=sqlite3.Row
        self.conn.execute('PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL')
        self.conn.execute('PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON')
        self.conn.execute('PRAGMA busy_timeout=30000')
        self._schema()
        if self.conn.execute('SELECT COUNT(*) FROM ledger').fetchone()[0]:
            if not self.verify_ledger()['valid']:
                raise IntegrityViolation('ledger integrity failure; refuse to write')
        else:
            self._seed()
        self.recover_interrupted()

    def _schema(self):
        self.conn.executescript('''
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS identities(id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, body TEXT NOT NULL);
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS memory(id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, owner_id TEXT NOT NULL, scope TEXT NOT NULL, kind TEXT NOT NULL,
          content TEXT NOT NULL, source_ref TEXT NOT NULL, ts TEXT NOT NULL);
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS grants(scope TEXT NOT NULL, recipient TEXT NOT NULL, owner_id TEXT NOT NULL, purpose TEXT NOT NULL,
          source_ref TEXT NOT NULL, ts TEXT NOT NULL, PRIMARY KEY(scope,recipient));
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS missions(id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, idem TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE, request_digest TEXT NOT NULL,
          body TEXT NOT NULL, status TEXT NOT NULL, answer TEXT, created_at TEXT NOT NULL, updated_at TEXT NOT NULL, receipt_id TEXT);
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS evidence(id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, body TEXT NOT NULL);
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS observations(id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, subject TEXT NOT NULL, source_ref TEXT NOT NULL, value TEXT NOT NULL,
          ts TEXT NOT NULL, body TEXT NOT NULL);
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS world_events(id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, seq INTEGER UNIQUE, body TEXT NOT NULL);
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS ledger(seq INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, created_at TEXT NOT NULL, kind TEXT NOT NULL,
          actor TEXT NOT NULL, subject TEXT NOT NULL, payload TEXT NOT NULL, prev_hash TEXT NOT NULL, hash TEXT NOT NULL);
        CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_observation_subject ON observations(subject,ts);
        CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_memory_scope ON memory(scope,ts);
        CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_mission_status ON missions(status,created_at);
        ''')

    @contextmanager
    def tx(self):
        with self.lock:
            self.conn.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
            try:
                yield
                self.conn.commit()
            except BaseException:
                self.conn.rollback()
                raise

    def close(self): self.conn.close()
    def _event(self,kind,actor,subject,payload):
        last=self.conn.execute('SELECT hash FROM ledger ORDER BY seq DESC LIMIT 1').fetchone()
        prev=last['hash'] if last else 'GENESIS'
        ts=now()
        body=canonical(payload)
        tag=hmac.new(self.audit_key,canonical([ts,kind,actor,subject,body,prev]).encode(),hashlib.sha256).hexdigest()
        self.conn.execute('INSERT INTO ledger(created_at,kind,actor,subject,payload,prev_hash,hash) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?)',
                          (ts,kind,actor,subject,body,prev,tag))
        return dict(seq=self.conn.execute('SELECT last_insert_rowid()').fetchone()[0],hash=tag,ts=ts)

    def verify_ledger(self):
        with self.lock:
            prev='GENESIS';count=0
            for row in self.conn.execute('SELECT * FROM ledger ORDER BY seq'):
                count+=1
                expected=hmac.new(self.audit_key,canonical([row['created_at'],row['kind'],row['actor'],row['subject'],row['payload'],prev]).encode(),hashlib.sha256).hexdigest()
                if not hmac.compare_digest(expected,row['hash']) or row['prev_hash']!=prev:
                    return {'valid':False,'events':count,'failed_seq':row['seq']}
                prev=row['hash']
            return {'valid':True,'events':count,'head':prev}

    def _seed(self):
        with self.tx():
            for i,(name,display,description,traits) in enumerate(MEMBER_INFO):
                record={
                    'intelligence_id':name,'display_name':display,'description':description,
                    'behavioral_constraints':traits,'lineage_version':'GrizOS_Dottie_v1.1_2026-10-04',
                    'identity_manifest_ref':f'grizos://intelligences/{name}',
                    'memory_namespace_ref':f'private:{name}',
                    'authority_profile_ref':'grizos://authority/operator-read-only-v1',
                    'relationship_state_ref':f'grizos://relationships/{name}',
                    'active_model_binding':None,'model_binding_history':[], 'worker_binding':None,
                    'lineage_source':'GrizOS_PRD_UI_Spatial_Dottie_v1.1; GMRI AEGIS founding roster',
                    'role':'governing_watch' if i==0 else 'aegis_member',
                }
                self.conn.execute('INSERT INTO identities(id,body) VALUES(?,?)',(name,canonical(record)))
            self._event('GENESIS','operator','grizos',{'roster':[x[0] for x in MEMBER_INFO],'source':'GMRI PRD v1.1'})

    def list_identities(self):
        with self.lock:
            return [json.loads(x['body']) for x in self.conn.execute('SELECT body FROM identities ORDER BY CASE WHEN id="dottie-hill" THEN 0 ELSE 1 END,id')]
    def identity(self,member):
        with self.lock:
            x=self.conn.execute('SELECT body FROM identities WHERE id=?',(member,)).fetchone()
        if not x:raise NotFound('intelligence not registered')
        return json.loads(x['body'])

    def bind_model(self,actor,member,binding,reason):
        if actor!='operator':raise AccessDenied('operator authority required')
        if not isinstance(binding,dict) or binding.get('provider') not in ('ollama','pi-durable'):
            raise InvalidState('unsupported model provider')
        if binding.get('provider')=='ollama':
            checked_text(binding.get('model'),'model',240)
        else:
            checked_text(binding.get('session'),'session',240)
        checked_text(binding.get('base_url'),'base_url',1000)
        reason=checked_text(reason,'reason',1000)
        with self.tx():
            m=self.identity(member)
            previous=m['active_model_binding']
            b=dict(binding);b['bound_at']=now();b['reason']=reason
            m['active_model_binding']=b
            m['model_binding_history'].append({'previous':previous,'new':b,'actor':actor})
            self.conn.execute('UPDATE identities SET body=? WHERE id=?',(canonical(m),member))
            self._event('MODEL_BINDING',actor,member,{'provider':b['provider'],'model':b.get('model'),'session':b.get('session'),'reason':reason})
            return m

    def _scope_allowed(self,actor,scope,write=False):
        self.identity(actor)
        if scope==f'private:{actor}': return True
        if scope.startswith('private:'):
            if write:return False
            row=self.conn.execute('SELECT 1 FROM grants WHERE scope=? AND recipient=?',(scope,actor)).fetchone()
            return bool(row)
        if scope=='institution:gmri':return actor=='dottie-hill'
        if scope=='aegis:shared':return actor.startswith('aegis-') or actor=='dottie-hill'
        if scope.startswith('mission:'):
            mid=scope[8:]
            m=self.conn.execute('SELECT body FROM missions WHERE id=?',(mid,)).fetchone()
            return bool(m and json.loads(m['body'])['delegatee_id']==actor)
        return False

    def remember(self,actor,scope,content,kind,source_ref):
        content=checked_text(content,'memory',20000)
        source_ref=checked_text(source_ref,'source_ref',1000)
        if kind not in MEMORY_KINDS:raise InvalidState('unknown memory kind')
        if kind=='FIRST_HAND' and not source_ref.startswith('event:'):
            raise AccessDenied('firsthand memory requires owned event provenance')
        with self.tx():
            if not self._scope_allowed(actor,scope,write=True):raise AccessDenied('memory scope write denied')
            mid=ident();ts=now()
            self.conn.execute('INSERT INTO memory VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?)',(mid,actor,scope,kind,content,source_ref,ts))
            self._event('MEMORY_WRITE',actor,scope,{'memory_id':mid,'kind':kind,'source_ref':source_ref,'sha256':digest(content)})
            return {'memory_id':mid,'scope':scope,'kind':kind,'source_ref':source_ref,'created_at':ts}

    def memories(self,actor,scope,limit=100):
        limit=max(1,min(int(limit),500))
        with self.lock:
            if not self._scope_allowed(actor,scope):raise AccessDenied('memory scope read denied')
            return [dict(row) for row in self.conn.execute('SELECT * FROM memory WHERE scope=? ORDER BY ts DESC LIMIT ?',(scope,limit))]

    def share(self,actor,scope,recipient,purpose,source_ref):
        if not scope.startswith('private:') or scope!=f'private:{actor}':raise AccessDenied('only owner may share private memory')
        self.identity(recipient)
        with self.tx():
            self.conn.execute('INSERT OR REPLACE INTO grants VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?)',
                   (scope,recipient,actor,checked_text(purpose,'purpose',1000),checked_text(source_ref,'source_ref',1000),now()))
            self._event('MEMORY_GRANT',actor,recipient,{'scope':scope,'purpose':purpose,'source_ref':source_ref})
            return {'scope':scope,'recipient':recipient,'purpose':purpose}

    def revoke(self,actor,scope,recipient):
        if scope!=f'private:{actor}':raise AccessDenied('only owner may revoke')
        with self.tx():
            self.conn.execute('DELETE FROM grants WHERE scope=? AND recipient=?',(scope,recipient))
            self._event('MEMORY_REVOKE',actor,recipient,{'scope':scope})

    def create_mission(self,actor,delegatee,intent,idempotency_key,action='INVESTIGATE',context_scopes=None,evidence_requirements=None):
        if actor!='operator' and actor!='dottie-hill':raise AccessDenied('principal is not authorized to dispatch')
        if actor=='dottie-hill' and (delegatee=='dottie-hill' or not delegatee.startswith('aegis-')):
            raise AccessDenied('Dottie may delegate only to AEGIS')
        if action not in ALLOWED_ACTIONS:raise AccessDenied('action outside default read-only envelope')
        self.identity(delegatee)
        intent=checked_text(intent,'intent',16000)
        idem=checked_text(idempotency_key,'idempotency key',200)
        context_scopes=context_scopes or []
        evidence_requirements=evidence_requirements or []
        if not isinstance(context_scopes,list) or not isinstance(evidence_requirements,list):raise InvalidState('scopes and evidence must be lists')
        for scope in context_scopes:
            if not self._scope_allowed(delegatee,scope,write=False):raise AccessDenied('context crosses identity boundary')
        body={'run_id':None,'mission_id':None,'principal_id':actor,'delegatee_id':delegatee,'intent':intent,
             'authority_lease':{'actions':[action],'mutation_targets':[],'principal':actor},
             'evidence_requirements':evidence_requirements,'idempotency_key':idem,'completion_criteria':['attributed_response_or_explicit_degradation'],
             'context_scope':context_scopes,'memory_read_scopes':context_scopes,'memory_write_policy':{'auto_promotion':False},'retry_budget':0,
             'deadline':None,'mutation_targets':[]}
        signature=digest(canonical(body))
        with self.tx():
            row=self.conn.execute('SELECT * FROM missions WHERE idem=?',(idem,)).fetchone()
            if row:
                if row['request_digest']!=signature:raise Conflict('idempotency key reused for a different request')
                return self._mission(row)
            mid=ident();ts=now();body['run_id']=mid;body['mission_id']=mid
            self.conn.execute('INSERT INTO missions VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)',(mid,idem,signature,canonical(body),'QUEUED',None,ts,ts,None))
            self._event('MISSION_QUEUED',actor,mid,{'delegatee':delegatee,'intent_sha256':digest(intent),'action':action})
            return self.get_mission(mid)

    def _mission(self,row):
        m=json.loads(row['body']);m.update(status=row['status'],answer=json.loads(row['answer']) if row['answer'] else None,
                                         created_at=row['created_at'],updated_at=row['updated_at'],receipt_id=row['receipt_id'])
        return m

    def get_mission(self,mid):
        with self.lock:
            r=self.conn.execute('SELECT * FROM missions WHERE id=?',(mid,)).fetchone()
        if not r:raise NotFound('mission unknown')
        return self._mission(r)

    def list_missions(self,limit=100):
        with self.lock:
            return [self._mission(x) for x in self.conn.execute('SELECT * FROM missions ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT ?',(max(1,min(int(limit),500)),))]
    def pending_missions(self):
        with self.lock:
            return [self._mission(x) for x in self.conn.execute('SELECT * FROM missions WHERE status="QUEUED" ORDER BY created_at')]

    def claim_mission(self,mid):
        with self.tx():
            r=self.conn.execute('SELECT status,body FROM missions WHERE id=?',(mid,)).fetchone()
            if not r:raise NotFound('mission unknown')
            if r['status']!='QUEUED':raise InvalidState('mission is not dispatchable')
            self.conn.execute('UPDATE missions SET status="RUNNING",updated_at=? WHERE id=?',(now(),mid))
            m=json.loads(r['body'])
            self._event('MISSION_DISPATCH_INTENT',m['principal_id'],mid,{'delegatee':m['delegatee_id'],'external_request_id':mid,'no_automatic_retry':True})
            return self.get_mission(mid)

    def finish_mission(self,mid,answer,source_ref):
        answer=checked_text(answer,'answer',64000)
        with self.tx():
            m=self.get_mission(mid)
            if m['status']!='RUNNING':raise InvalidState('mission not running')
            evidence=self._add_evidence(m['delegatee_id'],source_ref,answer,'ATTRIBUTED','model_output')
            result={'text':answer,'evidence_id':evidence['evidence_id'],'source_ref':source_ref}
            receipt=self._event('MISSION_DONE',m['delegatee_id'],mid,{'result_hash':digest(answer),'evidence_id':evidence['evidence_id'],'source_ref':source_ref})
            self.conn.execute('UPDATE missions SET status="DONE",answer=?,receipt_id=?,updated_at=? WHERE id=?',
                              (canonical(result),str(receipt['seq']),now(),mid))
            return self.get_mission(mid)

    def fail_mission(self,mid,reason,uncertain=True):
        with self.tx():
            m=self.get_mission(mid)
            if m['status'] not in ('RUNNING','QUEUED'):raise InvalidState('mission terminal')
            state='UNCERTAIN' if uncertain else 'BLOCKED'
            self.conn.execute('UPDATE missions SET status=?,answer=?,updated_at=? WHERE id=?',
                              (state,canonical({'error':checked_text(reason,'reason',1000)}),now(),mid))
            self._event('MISSION_'+state,'grizos',mid,{'reason':reason,'requires_reconciliation':uncertain})
            return self.get_mission(mid)

    def recover_interrupted(self):
        # Running requests may have reached external systems: do not resubmit.
        with self.tx():
            rows=self.conn.execute('SELECT id FROM missions WHERE status="RUNNING"').fetchall()
            for row in rows:
                self.conn.execute('UPDATE missions SET status="UNCERTAIN",answer=?,updated_at=? WHERE id=?',
                                  (canonical({'error':'interrupted while external outcome unknown'}),now(),row['id']))
                self._event('MISSION_RECOVERY_HOLD','grizos',row['id'],{'reason':'outcome unknown; no blind redispatch'})
            return len(rows)

    def reconcile(self,actor,mid,disposition,receipt_ref):
        if actor!='operator':raise AccessDenied('operator only')
        receipt_ref=checked_text(receipt_ref,'independent receipt reference',1000)
        if disposition not in ('confirmed_no_effect','confirmed_completed'):
            raise InvalidState('explicit external disposition required')
        with self.tx():
            m=self.get_mission(mid)
            if m['status']!='UNCERTAIN':raise InvalidState('only uncertain missions can reconcile')
            # Never convert unverified external execution to an internally-generated DONE result.
            state='QUEUED' if disposition=='confirmed_no_effect' else 'RECONCILED_EXTERNAL'
            self.conn.execute('UPDATE missions SET status=?,answer=?,updated_at=? WHERE id=?',
                (state,canonical({'reconciliation':disposition,'receipt_ref':receipt_ref}),now(),mid))
            self._event('MISSION_RECONCILED',actor,mid,{'disposition':disposition,'receipt_ref':receipt_ref})
            return self.get_mission(mid)

    def _add_evidence(self,actor,source_ref,content,evidence_state,source_class,observed_at=None,provenance=None):
        self.identity(actor)
        source_ref=checked_text(source_ref,'source_ref',1000)
        content=checked_text(content,'content',64000)
        if evidence_state not in EVIDENCE_STATES:raise InvalidState('invalid evidence state')
        if not re.fullmatch(r'[a-z][a-z0-9_-]{0,63}',source_class):raise InvalidState('invalid source class')
        observed_at=observed_at or now()
        eid=ident()
        record={'evidence_id':eid,'source_ref':source_ref,'source_class':source_class,'observed_at':observed_at,
                'content_hash':'sha256:'+digest(content),'provenance':provenance or {'actor_id':actor,'observed_by':'gmri-dottie-runtime'},
                'evidence_state':evidence_state,'transformations':[], 'content':content}
        self.conn.execute('INSERT INTO evidence(id,body) VALUES(?,?)',(eid,canonical(record)))
        self._event('EVIDENCE_RECORDED',actor,eid,{'source_ref':source_ref,'evidence_state':evidence_state,'content_hash':record['content_hash']})
        return record

    def add_evidence(self,actor,source_ref,content,evidence_state='UNVERIFIED',source_class='external',observed_at=None,provenance=None):
        with self.tx(): return self._add_evidence(actor,source_ref,content,evidence_state,source_class,observed_at,provenance)

    def list_evidence(self,limit=100):
        with self.lock:
            return [json.loads(x['body']) for x in self.conn.execute('SELECT body FROM evidence ORDER BY rowid DESC LIMIT ?', (max(1,min(int(limit),500)),))]

    def observe(self,actor,subject,value,source_ref,severity='routine',materiality='low',observed_at=None,source_class='external',license='unknown'):
        subject=checked_text(subject,'subject',200)
        value=checked_text(value,'observation',10000)
        source_ref=checked_text(source_ref,'source_ref',1000)
        if severity not in SEVERITIES or materiality not in MATERIALITIES:raise InvalidState('unknown severity or materiality')
        ts=observed_at or now()
        # Preserve clocks and source claims; no automatic claim of primary verification.
        with self.tx():
            self.identity(actor)
            last=self.conn.execute('SELECT * FROM observations WHERE subject=? ORDER BY rowid DESC LIMIT 1',(subject,)).fetchone()
            same_source=self.conn.execute('SELECT * FROM observations WHERE subject=? AND source_ref=? ORDER BY rowid DESC LIMIT 1',(subject,source_ref)).fetchone()
            unchanged=bool(same_source and same_source['value']==value)
            conflicting=bool(last and last['source_ref']!=source_ref and last['value']!=value)
            score=SEVERITIES[severity]+MATERIALITIES[materiality]
            if unchanged: decision='HOLD';reason='unchanged'
            elif score>=4:decision='INTERRUPT';reason='material high-consequence change'
            elif score>=2:decision='BRIEF';reason='material change; bounded attention'
            else:decision='HOLD';reason='below interruption threshold'
            if conflicting and score>=4:reason='high-consequence conflicting sources; not resolved'
            evidence_state='CONTRADICTED' if conflicting else 'UNVERIFIED'
            obj={'id':ident(),'subject':subject,'value':value,'source_ref':source_ref,
                 'source_class':source_class,'license':license,'observed_at':ts,'ingested_at':now(),
                 'severity':severity,'materiality':materiality,'decision':decision,'reason':reason,
                 'conflict':conflicting,'evidence_state':evidence_state,'previous_value':last['value'] if last else None}
            self.conn.execute('INSERT INTO observations VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?)',(obj['id'],subject,source_ref,value,ts,canonical(obj)))
            ev=self._event('WATCH_OBSERVATION',actor,subject,{'observation_id':obj['id'],'source_ref':source_ref,'decision':decision,'conflict':conflicting})
            self._world_event(actor,'watch.observation',{'subject':subject,'decision':decision,'conflict':conflicting,'observation_id':obj['id']},[source_ref],'EXTERNAL_OBSERVATION')
            obj['receipt_seq']=ev['seq'];return obj

    def observations(self,limit=100):
        with self.lock:
            return [json.loads(x['body']) for x in self.conn.execute('SELECT body FROM observations ORDER BY rowid DESC LIMIT ?',(max(1,min(int(limit),500)),))]

    def _world_event(self,actor,event_type,payload,source_refs,canonicality):
        if canonicality not in ('CANONICAL','PROJECTION_ONLY','CANDIDATE','EXTERNAL_OBSERVATION'):
            raise InvalidState('bad canonicality')
        eid=ident();ts=now()
        event={'event_id':eid,'event_type':event_type,'actor_id':actor,'observed_at':ts,'source_refs':source_refs,
               'canonicality':canonicality,'payload':payload}
        self._event('WORLD_EVENT',actor,eid,{'event_type':event_type,'canonicality':canonicality,'source_refs':source_refs})
        seq=self.conn.execute('SELECT last_insert_rowid()').fetchone()[0]
        self.conn.execute('INSERT INTO world_events VALUES(?,?,?)',(eid,seq,canonical(event)))
        return event

    def world_events(self,after=0,limit=200):
        with self.lock:
            rows=self.conn.execute('SELECT seq,body FROM world_events WHERE seq>? ORDER BY seq LIMIT ?',
                                   (max(0,int(after)),max(1,min(int(limit),500)))).fetchall()
            return {'events':[json.loads(x['body']) for x in rows],'cursor':rows[-1]['seq'] if rows else int(after),
                    'canonical_source':'grizos-dottie-runtime','projection_only':True}

    def ledger(self,after=0,limit=100):
        with self.lock:
            return [dict(x) for x in self.conn.execute('SELECT seq,created_at,kind,actor,subject,prev_hash,hash FROM ledger WHERE seq>? ORDER BY seq LIMIT ?',
                                                      (int(after),max(1,min(int(limit),500))))]
    def backup(self,target):
        target=Path(target).resolve()
        if target==self.path or target.exists():raise InvalidState('backup must use a new path')
        target.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
        with self.lock:
            if not self.verify_ledger()['valid']:raise IntegrityViolation('ledger invalid')
            with sqlite3.connect(str(target)) as dst:self.conn.backup(dst)
        os.chmod(target,0o600)
        return {'path':str(target),'size_bytes':target.stat().st_size,'sha256':hashlib.sha256(target.read_bytes()).hexdigest()}

    assert c.get('/v1/memory/private:aegis-morita',headers=MORITA).status_code==200

def test_inference_unconfigured_is_blocked_not_faked(service):

class PiStub(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    calls=[]
    readonly=True
    def log_message(self,*args):pass
    def do_GET(self):
        if self.path.endswith('/agents/main'):
            return self._send(200,{'config':{'readOnly':type(self).readonly},'tools':[{'name':'read','offered':True}]})
        return self._send(404,{'error':{'code':'not_found'}})
    def do_POST(self):
        body=json.loads(self.rfile.read(int(self.headers['Content-Length'])))
        type(self).calls.append(body)
        if '/messages' in self.path:
            return self._send(200,{'status':'done','answer':{'entryId':99,'text':'Attributed Pi inference'}})
        return self._send(404,{'error':{'code':'not_found'}})
    def _send(self,status,payload):
        body=json.dumps(payload).encode()
        self.send_response(status);self.send_header('Content-Type','application/json');self.send_header('Content-Length',str(len(body)));self.end_headers();self.wfile.write(body)
