            self._event('COLLABORATION_'+status,'dottie-hill',cid,{'mission_ids':mission_ids,'summary_evidence_id':summary_evidence_id})
            return obj

    def create_mission(self,actor,delegatee,intent,idempotency_key,action='INVESTIGATE',context_scopes=None,evidence_requirements=None):
        if actor!='operator' and actor!='dottie-hill':raise AccessDenied('principal is not authorized to dispatch')
        if actor=='dottie-hill' and (delegatee=='dottie-hill' or not delegatee.startswith('aegis-')):
            raise AccessDenied('Dottie may delegate only to AEGIS')
        if action not in ALLOWED_ACTIONS:raise AccessDenied('action outside default read-only envelope')
        self.identity(delegatee)
        intent=checked_text(intent,'intent',16000)
        idem=checked_text(idempotency_key,'idempotency key',200)
        context_scopes=context_scopes or []
        evidence_requirements=evidence_requirements or []
        if not isinstance(context_scopes,list) or not isinstance(evidence_requirements,list):raise InvalidState('scopes and evidence must be lists')
        for scope in context_scopes:
            if not self._scope_allowed(delegatee,scope,write=False):raise AccessDenied('context crosses identity boundary')
        body={'run_id':None,'mission_id':None,'principal_id':actor,'delegatee_id':delegatee,'intent':intent,
             'authority_lease':{'actions':[action],'mutation_targets':[],'principal':actor},
             'evidence_requirements':evidence_requirements,'idempotency_key':idem,'completion_criteria':['attributed_response_or_explicit_degradation'],
             'context_scope':context_scopes,'memory_read_scopes':context_scopes,'memory_write_policy':{'auto_promotion':False},'retry_budget':0,
             'deadline':None,'mutation_targets':[]}
        signature=digest(canonical(body))
        with self.tx():
            row=self.conn.execute('SELECT * FROM missions WHERE idem=?',(idem,)).fetchone()
            if row:
                if row['request_digest']!=signature:raise Conflict('idempotency key reused for a different request')
                return self._mission(row)
            mid=ident();ts=now();body['run_id']=mid;body['mission_id']=mid
            self.conn.execute('INSERT INTO missions VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)',(mid,idem,signature,canonical(body),'QUEUED',None,ts,ts,None))
            self._event('MISSION_QUEUED',actor,mid,{'delegatee':delegatee,'intent_sha256':digest(intent),'action':action})
            return self.get_mission(mid)

    def _mission(self,row):
        m=json.loads(row['body']);m.update(status=row['status'],answer=json.loads(row['answer']) if row['answer'] else None,
                                         created_at=row['created_at'],updated_at=row['updated_at'],receipt_id=row['receipt_id'])
        return m

    def get_mission(self,mid):
        with self.lock:
            r=self.conn.execute('SELECT * FROM missions WHERE id=?',(mid,)).fetchone()
        if not r:raise NotFound('mission unknown')
        return self._mission(r)

    def list_missions(self,limit=100):
        with self.lock:
            return [self._mission(x) for x in self.conn.execute('SELECT * FROM missions ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT ?',(max(1,min(int(limit),500)),))]
    def pending_missions(self):
        with self.lock:
            return [self._mission(x) for x in self.conn.execute('SELECT * FROM missions WHERE status="QUEUED" ORDER BY created_at')]

    def claim_mission(self,mid):
        with self.tx():
            r=self.conn.execute('SELECT status,body FROM missions WHERE id=?',(mid,)).fetchone()
            if not r:raise NotFound('mission unknown')
            if r['status']!='QUEUED':raise InvalidState('mission is not dispatchable')
            self.conn.execute('UPDATE missions SET status="RUNNING",updated_at=? WHERE id=?',(now(),mid))
            m=json.loads(r['body'])
            self._event('MISSION_DISPATCH_INTENT',m['principal_id'],mid,{'delegatee':m['delegatee_id'],'external_request_id':mid,'no_automatic_retry':True})
            return self.get_mission(mid)

    def finish_mission(self,mid,answer,source_ref):
        answer=checked_text(answer,'answer',64000)
        with self.tx():
            m=self.get_mission(mid)
            if m['status']!='RUNNING':raise InvalidState('mission not running')
            evidence=self._add_evidence(m['delegatee_id'],source_ref,answer,'ATTRIBUTED','model_output')
            result={'text':answer,'evidence_id':evidence['evidence_id'],'source_ref':source_ref}
            receipt=self._event('MISSION_DONE',m['delegatee_id'],mid,{'result_hash':digest(answer),'evidence_id':evidence['evidence_id'],'source_ref':source_ref})
            self.conn.execute('UPDATE missions SET status="DONE",answer=?,receipt_id=?,updated_at=? WHERE id=?',
                              (canonical(result),str(receipt['seq']),now(),mid))
            return self.get_mission(mid)

    def fail_mission(self,mid,reason,uncertain=True):
        with self.tx():
            m=self.get_mission(mid)
            if m['status'] not in ('RUNNING','QUEUED'):raise InvalidState('mission terminal')
            state='UNCERTAIN' if uncertain else 'BLOCKED'
            self.conn.execute('UPDATE missions SET status=?,answer=?,updated_at=? WHERE id=?',
                              (state,canonical({'error':checked_text(reason,'reason',1000)}),now(),mid))
            self._event('MISSION_'+state,'grizos',mid,{'reason':reason,'requires_reconciliation':uncertain})
            return self.get_mission(mid)

    def recover_interrupted(self):
        # Running requests may have reached external systems: do not resubmit.
        with self.tx():
            rows=self.conn.execute('SELECT id FROM missions WHERE status="RUNNING"').fetchall()
            for row in rows:
                self.conn.execute('UPDATE missions SET status="UNCERTAIN",answer=?,updated_at=? WHERE id=?',
                                  (canonical({'error':'interrupted while external outcome unknown'}),now(),row['id']))
                self._event('MISSION_RECOVERY_HOLD','grizos',row['id'],{'reason':'outcome unknown; no blind redispatch'})
            return len(rows)

    def reconcile(self,actor,mid,disposition,receipt_ref):
        if actor!='operator':raise AccessDenied('operator only')
        receipt_ref=checked_text(receipt_ref,'independent receipt reference',1000)
        if disposition not in ('confirmed_no_effect','confirmed_completed'):
            raise InvalidState('explicit external disposition required')
        with self.tx():
            m=self.get_mission(mid)
            if m['status']!='UNCERTAIN':raise InvalidState('only uncertain missions can reconcile')
            # Never convert unverified external execution to an internally-generated DONE result.
            state='QUEUED' if disposition=='confirmed_no_effect' else 'RECONCILED_EXTERNAL'
            self.conn.execute('UPDATE missions SET status=?,answer=?,updated_at=? WHERE id=?',
                (state,canonical({'reconciliation':disposition,'receipt_ref':receipt_ref}),now(),mid))
            self._event('MISSION_RECONCILED',actor,mid,{'disposition':disposition,'receipt_ref':receipt_ref})
            return self.get_mission(mid)

    def _add_evidence(self,actor,source_ref,content,evidence_state,source_class,observed_at=None,provenance=None):
        if actor!='operator':self.identity(actor)
        source_ref=checked_text(source_ref,'source_ref',1000)

            raise AccessDenied('primary verification requires operator authority and independent verification receipt')

        observed_at=observed_at or now()

                'content_hash':'sha256:'+digest(content),'provenance':provenance or {'actor_id':actor,'observed_by':'gmri-dottie-runtime'},
                'evidence_state':evidence_state,'transformations':[], 'content':content}
        self.conn.execute('INSERT INTO evidence(id,body) VALUES(?,?)',(eid,canonical(record)))
        self._event('EVIDENCE_RECORDED',actor,eid,{'source_ref':source_ref,'evidence_state':evidence_state,'content_hash':record['content_hash']})
        return record

    def add_evidence(self,actor,source_ref,content,evidence_state='UNVERIFIED',source_class='external',observed_at=None,provenance=None):
        with self.tx(): return self._add_evidence(actor,source_ref,content,evidence_state,source_class,observed_at,provenance)

    def observe(self,actor,subject,value,source_ref,severity='routine',materiality='low',observed_at=None,source_class='external',license='unknown',ttl_seconds=86400):
        subject=checked_text(subject,'subject',200)
        value=checked_text(value,'observation',10000)
        source_ref=checked_text(source_ref,'source_ref',1000)
        if severity not in SEVERITIES or materiality not in MATERIALITIES:raise InvalidState('unknown severity or materiality')
        ts=observed_at or now()
        if not isinstance(ttl_seconds,int) or not 1<=ttl_seconds<=365*86400:
            raise InvalidState('ttl_seconds outside allowed range')
        try:
            timestamp=datetime.fromisoformat(ts.replace('Z','+00:00'))
            if timestamp.tzinfo is None:raise ValueError('timezone required')
            age=(datetime.now(timezone.utc)-timestamp.astimezone(timezone.utc)).total_seconds()
        except (ValueError,TypeError):raise InvalidState('observed_at must be timezone-aware ISO8601') from None
        stale=age>ttl_seconds or age < -300
        # Preserve clocks and source claims; no automatic claim of primary verification.
        with self.tx():
            if actor!='operator':self.identity(actor)
            last=self.conn.execute('SELECT * FROM observations WHERE subject=? ORDER BY rowid DESC LIMIT 1',(subject,)).fetchone()
            same_source=self.conn.execute('SELECT * FROM observations WHERE subject=? AND source_ref=? ORDER BY rowid DESC LIMIT 1',(subject,source_ref)).fetchone()
            unchanged=bool(same_source and same_source['value']==value)
            conflicting=bool(last and last['source_ref']!=source_ref and last['value']!=value)
            score=SEVERITIES[severity]+MATERIALITIES[materiality]
            if stale:decision='HOLD';reason='source observation stale or future-dated; no current-state claim'
            elif unchanged: decision='HOLD';reason='unchanged'
            elif score>=4:decision='INTERRUPT';reason='material high-consequence change'
            elif score>=2:decision='BRIEF';reason='material change; bounded attention'
            else:decision='HOLD';reason='below interruption threshold'
            if conflicting and score>=4 and not stale:reason='high-consequence conflicting sources; not resolved'
            evidence_state='CONTRADICTED' if conflicting else 'UNVERIFIED'
            obj={'id':ident(),'subject':subject,'value':value,'source_ref':source_ref,
                 'source_class':source_class,'license':license,'observed_at':ts,'ingested_at':now(),
                 'severity':severity,'materiality':materiality,'decision':decision,'reason':reason,
                 'stale':stale,'ttl_seconds':ttl_seconds,'conflict':conflicting,
                 'evidence_state':evidence_state,'previous_value':last['value'] if last else None}
            self.conn.execute('INSERT INTO observations VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?)',(obj['id'],subject,source_ref,value,ts,canonical(obj)))
            ev=self._event('WATCH_OBSERVATION',actor,subject,{'observation_id':obj['id'],'source_ref':source_ref,'decision':decision,'conflict':conflicting})
            self._world_event(actor,'watch.observation',{'subject':subject,'decision':decision,'conflict':conflicting,'observation_id':obj['id']},[source_ref],'EXTERNAL_OBSERVATION')
            obj['receipt_seq']=ev['seq'];return obj

    def register_source(self,actor,config):
        if actor!='operator':raise AccessDenied('operator authority required to add a source')
        if not isinstance(config,dict):raise InvalidState('invalid source configuration')
        fields={'source_id','url','field','subject','severity','materiality','source_class','license','interval_seconds','ttl_seconds','timestamp_field'}
        if set(config)-fields:raise InvalidState('unexpected source configuration field')
        mandatory=fields-{'timestamp_field'}
        if mandatory-set(config):raise InvalidState('source requires explicit attribution, timing, and license')
        from .watch_sources import validate_source_url
        try:validate_source_url(config['url'])
        except ValueError as ex:raise InvalidState(str(ex)) from None
        key=checked_text(config['source_id'],'source_id',100)
        checked_text(config['subject'],'subject',200)
        path=checked_text(config['field'],'field',300)
        if not re.fullmatch(r'[A-Za-z0-9_-]+(\.[A-Za-z0-9_-]+)*',path):raise InvalidState('source field must be a simple JSON key path')
        if config.get('timestamp_field'):
            if not re.fullmatch(r'[A-Za-z0-9_-]+(\.[A-Za-z0-9_-]+)*',config['timestamp_field']):
                raise InvalidState('timestamp field must be a JSON key path')
        if config['severity'] not in SEVERITIES or config['materiality'] not in MATERIALITIES:raise InvalidState('invalid watch severity')
        if not isinstance(config['interval_seconds'],int) or config['interval_seconds']<60:raise InvalidState('minimum polling interval is 60 seconds')
        if not isinstance(config['ttl_seconds'],int) or not 1<=config['ttl_seconds']<=365*86400:raise InvalidState('invalid TTL')
        checked_text(config['source_class'],'source_class',64)
        checked_text(config['license'],'license',200)
        body={**config,'last_status':'NEVER_CHECKED','last_error':None,'last_checked_at':None,'next_due':0}
        with self.tx():
            row=self.conn.execute('SELECT body FROM watch_sources WHERE source_id=?',(key,)).fetchone()
            if row:
                existing=json.loads(row['body'])
                immutable={k:v for k,v in existing.items() if k in fields}
                if immutable!=config:raise Conflict('source ID already configured differently; no silent override')
                return existing
            self.conn.execute('INSERT INTO watch_sources(source_id,body) VALUES(?,?)',(key,canonical(body)))
            self._event('SOURCE_REGISTERED',actor,key,{'url_host':urlsplit(config['url']).hostname,'source_class':config['source_class'],'license':config['license']})
        return body

    def sources(self):
        with self.lock:return [json.loads(r['body']) for r in self.conn.execute('SELECT body FROM watch_sources ORDER BY source_id')]

    def source(self,source_id):
        with self.lock:r=self.conn.execute('SELECT body FROM watch_sources WHERE source_id=?',(source_id,)).fetchone()
        if not r:raise NotFound('watch source does not exist')
        return json.loads(r['body'])

    def update_source_status(self,source_id,status,error=None):
        if status not in ('OK','DEGRADED','POLLING'):raise InvalidState('invalid source status')
        with self.tx():
            r=self.source(source_id)
            r.update(last_status=status,last_error=error,last_checked_at=now(),next_due=time.time()+r['interval_seconds'])
            self.conn.execute('UPDATE watch_sources SET body=? WHERE source_id=?',(canonical(r),source_id))
            self._event('SOURCE_POLL_STATUS','grizos',source_id,{'status':status,'error_type':error})
            return r

    def observations(self,limit=100):
        with self.lock:
            return [json.loads(x['body']) for x in self.conn.execute('SELECT body FROM observations ORDER BY rowid DESC LIMIT ?',(max(1,min(int(limit),500)),))]

    def _world_event(self,actor,event_type,payload,source_refs,canonicality):
        if canonicality not in ('CANONICAL','PROJECTION_ONLY','CANDIDATE','EXTERNAL_OBSERVATION'):
            raise InvalidState('bad canonicality')
        eid=ident();ts=now()
        event={'event_id':eid,'event_type':event_type,'actor_id':actor,'observed_at':ts,'source_refs':source_refs,
               'canonicality':canonicality,'payload':payload}
        self._event('WORLD_EVENT',actor,eid,{'event_type':event_type,'c[... ELLIPSIZATION ...]True,mode=0o700)
    if not root.is_dir():raise InvalidState('state path is not directory')
    sec=root/'secrets'
    required=[sec/'audit.key',sec/'operator.token',sec/'projection.token']+[sec/'agents'/(m[0]+'.token') for m in MEMBER_INFO]
    existing=[p.exists() for p in required]
    db=root/'state.sqlite'
    if all(existing) and db.exists():
        core,_=load_runtime(root)
        try:core.verify_ledger()
        finally:core.close()
        return {'initialized':False,'state':str(root),'operator_token_file':str(sec/'operator.token'),'secrets_preserved':True}
    if any(existing) or db.exists():
        raise InvalidState('partial/existing state detected: refusing to overwrite or regenerate credentials')
    sec.mkdir(mode=0o700);(sec/'agents').mkdir(mode=0o700)
    _write_new(sec/'audit.key',secrets.token_bytes(32))
    _write_new(sec/'operator.token',secrets.token_urlsafe(48).encode()+b'\n')
    _write_new(sec/'projection.token',secrets.token_urlsafe(48).encode()+b'\n')
    for i in MEMBER_INFO:_write_new(sec/'agents'/(i[0]+'.token'),secrets.token_urlsafe(48).encode()+b'\n')
    core=Core(db,(sec/'audit.key').read_bytes())
    core.close()
    os.chmod(db,0o600)
    return {'initialized':True,'state':str(root),'operator_token_file':str(sec/'operator.token'),
            'secrets_preserved':False,'production_field_accepted':False}


def load_runtime(root:Path):
    root=Path(root).expanduser().resolve()
    sec=root/'secrets'
    if not (root/'state.sqlite').is_file() or not (sec/'audit.key').is_file():
        raise InvalidState('state or signing key not initialized; refuse replacement')
    audit=(sec/'audit.key').read_bytes()
    if len(audit)<32:raise InvalidState('audit key is invalid')
    operator=(sec/'operator.token').read_text().strip()
    projection=(sec/'projection.token').read_text().strip()
    agents={m[0]:(sec/'agents'/(m[0]+'.token')).read_text().strip() for m in MEMBER_INFO}
    if not operator or not projection or any(not x for x in agents.values()):
        raise InvalidState('missing principal token')
    if (sec/'pi.token').is_file() and not os.environ.get('DOTTIE_PI_TOKEN'):
        os.environ['DOTTIE_PI_TOKEN']=(sec/'pi.token').read_text().strip()
    c=Core(root/'state.sqlite',audit)
    return c,{'operator':operator,'projection':projection,'agents':agents}


def main(argv=None):
    parser=argparse.ArgumentParser(prog='dottie-hill',description='GMRI Dottie Hill sovereign runtime')
    sub=parser.add_subparsers(dest='command',required=True)
    for command in ('init','serve','status','verify','backup','verify-backup','bind-model','run-pending'):
        p=sub.add_parser(command)
        p.add_argument('--data',default=os.environ.get('DOTTIE_DATA','./dottie-state'))
        if command=='serve':
            p.add_argument('--host',default='127.0.0.1')
            p.add_argument('--port',type=int,default=8768)
        if command in ('backup','verify-backup'):p.add_argument('--target',required=True)
        if command=='bind-model':
            p.add_argument('--member',required=True)
            p.add_argument('--provider',required=True,choices=['pi-durable','ollama'])
            p.add_argument('--base-url',required=True)
            p.add_argument('--model');p.add_argument('--session');p.add_argument('--agent')
            p.add_argument('--reason',required=True)
    args=parser.parse_args(argv)
    try:
        if args.command=='init':
            print(json.dumps(initialize(Path(args.data)),indent=2));return 0
        c,creds=load_runtime(Path(args.data))
        try:
            if args.command=='serve':
                if args.host not in ('127.0.0.1','localhost','::1'):
                    raise InvalidState('only loopback bind permitted; use existing authenticated TLS/NetBird ingress')
                lockfile=Path(args.data).expanduser().resolve()/'run.lock'
                handle=lockfile.open('a+')
                try:fcntl.flock(handle,fcntl.LOCK_EX|fcntl.LOCK_NB)
                except BlockingIOError:raise InvalidState('service already running with exclusive state lock')
                # Recovery only under the exclusive production process lease.
                c.recover_interrupted()
                import uvicorn
                app=create_app(c,creds['operator'],creds['agents'],creds['projection'],background=True)
                uvicorn.run(app,host=args.host,port=args.port,workers=1,log_level='warning',access_log=False)
                return 0
            if args.command=='status':
                ids=c.list_identities()
                print(json.dumps({'registered':[x['intelligence_id'] for x in ids],
                    'configured_models':{x['intelligence_id']:bool(x['active_model_binding']) for x in ids},
                    'mission_count':len(c.list_missions()),'ledger':c.verify_ledger(),
                    'production_field_accepted':False,'live_substrate_verified':False},indent=2));return 0
            if args.command=='verify':
                result=c.verify_ledger();print(json.dumps(result,indent=2));return 0 if result['valid'] else 2
            if args.command=='backup':
                print(json.dumps(c.backup(args.target),indent=2));return 0
            if args.command=='verify-backup':
                b=Core(Path(args.target),c.audit_key)
                try:result=b.verify_ledger()
                finally:b.close()
                print(json.dumps(result,indent=2));return 0 if result['valid'] else 2
            if args.command=='bind-model':
                binding={'provider':args.provider,'base_url':args.base_url}
                if args.model:binding['model']=args.model
                if args.session:binding['session']=args.session
                if args.agent:binding['agent']=args.agent
                m=c.bind_model('operator',args.member,binding,args.reason)
                print(json.dumps({'bound':m['intelligence_id'],'binding':m['active_model_binding'],'production_field_accepted':False},indent=2));return 0
            if args.command=='run-pending':
                from .service import AgentService
                results=AgentService(c).process_pending(limit=20)
                print(json.dumps([{'mission_id':m['mission_id'],'status':m['status']} for m in results],indent=2));return 0
        finally:c.close()
    except (InvalidState,ValueError,OSError) as e:
        print(json.dumps({'error':str(e),'success':False}),file=sys.stderr);return 2
    return 2

if __name__=='__main__':raise SystemExit(main())

"""Polling adapter for explicitly authorized read-only JSON sources."""
from __future__ import annotations
import json
import time
from urllib.parse import urlsplit
from urllib.request import Request, build_opener
from urllib.error import URLError,HTTPError
from .provider import _NoRedirect, PreflightFailure
from .core import checked_text, InvalidState


def validate_source_url(url):
    if not isinstance(url,str):raise ValueError('source URL required')
    parsed=urlsplit(url)
    if parsed.scheme not in ('http','https') or not parsed.hostname or parsed.username or parsed.password or parsed.query or parsed.fragment:
        raise ValueError('source requires explicit uncredentialed HTTP(S) URL without query/fragment')
    if parsed.scheme=='http' and parsed.hostname not in ('127.0.0.1','::1','localhost'):
        raise ValueError('remote source must use HTTPS')
    if len(url)>1500:raise ValueError('source URL too long')
    return url


def _field(data,path):
    for token in path.split('.'):
        if isinstance(data,dict):data=data[token]
        elif isinstance(data,list) and token.isdigit():data=data[int(token)]
        else:raise ValueError('JSON field does not exist')
    if isinstance(data,(dict,list)):
        data=json.dumps(data,sort_keys=True,separators=(',',':'))
    if data is None:raise ValueError('JSON field is null')
    return str(data)

class WatchPoller:
    def __init__(self,core,timeout=10):
        self.core=core;self.timeout=timeout;self.opener=build_opener(_NoRedirect())
    def poll(self,source_id):
        source=self.core.source(source_id)
        self.core.update_source_status(source_id,'POLLING')
        try:
            url=validate_source_url(source['url'])
            with self.opener.open(Request(url,headers={'Accept':'application/json','User-Agent':'GMRI-Watch/0.1'}),timeout=self.timeout) as response:
                if response.status!=200:raise ValueError('upstream did not respond 200')
                raw=response.read(512001)
                if len(raw)>512000:raise ValueError('response larger than 500 KiB')
                data=json.loads(raw)
            value=checked_text(_field(data,source['field']),'source observation',10000)
            stamp=_field(data,source['timestamp_field']) if source.get('timestamp_field') else None
            observation=self.core.observe('dottie-hill',source['subject'],value,source_id,source['severity'],source['materiality'],
                                          observed_at=stamp,source_class=source['source_class'],license=source['license'],ttl_seconds=source['ttl_seconds'])
            self.core.update_source_status(source_id,'OK')
            return {'source_id':source_id,'status':'OK','decision':observation['decision'],'observation_id':observation['id']}
        except (URLError,HTTPError,TimeoutError,OSError,ValueError,KeyError,IndexError,InvalidState,PreflightFailure,json.JSONDecodeError) as e:
            # Never write raw upstream payload or untrusted error text to durable audit.
            reason=type(e).__name__
            self.core.update_source_status(source_id,'DEGRADED',reason)
            return {'source_id':source_id,'status':'DEGRADED','error_type':reason,'observation_created':False}

    def poll_due(self,limit=4):
        out=[];instant=time.time()
        for s in self.core.sources():
            if len(out)>=limit:break
            if s['next_due']<=instant:out.append(self.poll(s['source_id']))
        return out

"""Authenticated GrizOS capability gateway, not a standalone replacement for GrizOS."""
from __future__ import annotations
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
from hashlib import sha256
from hmac import compare_digest
from pathlib import Path
import threading
import time
from fastapi import Depends, FastAPI, Header, HTTPException, Request
from fastapi.responses import FileResponse
from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field
from .core import Core, AccessDenied, Conflict, InvalidState, IntegrityViolation, NotFound, ALLOWED_ACTIONS
from .provider import ModelRouter, PreflightFailure, OutcomeUncertain
from .watch_sources import WatchPoller

class Strict(BaseModel):model_config=ConfigDict(extra='forbid')
class MissionInput(Strict):
    delegatee:str
    intent:str=Field(min_length=1,max_length=16000)
    idempotency_key:str=Field(min_length=1,max_length=200)
    action:str='INVESTIGATE'
    context_scopes:list[str]=Field(default_factory=list)
    evidence_requirements:list[str]=Field(default_factory=list)
class BindingInput(Strict):
    provider:str
    base_url:str
    reason:str
    model:str|None=None
    session:str|None=None
    agent:str|None=None
class MemoryInput(Strict):
    scope:str
    content:str
    kind:str
    source_ref:str
class ShareInput(Strict):
    scope:str
    recipient:str
    purpose:str
    source_ref:str
class ObserveInput(Strict):
    subject:str
    value:str
    source_ref:str
    severity:str='routine'
    materiality:str='low'
    observed_at:str|None=None
    source_class:str='external'
    license:str='unknown'
class EvidenceInput(Strict):
    source_ref:str
    content:str
    evidence_state:str='UNVERIFIED'
    source_class:str='external'
class ReconcileInput(Strict):
    disposition:str
    receipt_ref:str
class CollaborationInput(Strict):
    intent:str
    participants:list[str]
    idempotency_key:str
class SourceInput(Strict):
    source_id:str
    url:str
    field:str
    subject:str
    severity:str='routine'
    materiality:str='low'
    source_class:str='external'
    license:str
    interval_seconds:int=60
    ttl_seconds:int=3600
    timestamp_field:str|None=None

class AgentService:
    def __init__(self,core:Core):
        self.core=core
        self.router=ModelRouter(core)
        self._lock=threading.RLock()

    def run(self,mid):
        with self._lock:
            m=self.core.claim_mission(mid)
        try:
            result=self.router.invoke(m)
        except PreflightFailure as exc:
            return self.core.fail_mission(mid,str(exc),uncertain=False)
        except OutcomeUncertain as exc:
            return self.core.fail_mission(mid,str(exc),uncertain=True)
        except Exception as exc:
            # A network operation may have reached a provider even if its parser crashed.
            return self.core.fail_mission(mid,f'unexpected provider failure ({type(exc).__name__}); outcome unknown',uncertain=True)
        return self.core.finish_mission(mid,result['text'],result['source_ref'])

    def collaborate(self,cid):
        with self._lock:
            group=self.core.get_collaboration(cid)
            if group['status']=='DONE':return group
            ids=list(group['mission_ids'])
            for member in group['participants']:
                key=f"collaboration:{cid}:{member}"
                work=self.core.create_mission('dottie-hill',member,group['intent'],key,
                    evidence_requirements=['source_attribution','independent_assessment'])
                if work['mission_id'] not in ids:ids.append(work['mission_id'])
            group=self.core.update_collaboration(cid,'RUNNING',ids)
            for mid in list(ids):
                work=self.core.get_mission(mid)
                if work['status']=='QUEUED':
                    try:self.run(mid)
                    except InvalidState:pass
            responses=[self.core.get_mission(mid) for mid in ids]
            if any(x['status']!='DONE' for x in responses):
                return self.core.update_collaboration(cid,'PARTIAL',ids)
            if len(responses)>len(group['participants']):
                synthesis=responses[-1]
            else:
                # Keep agent outputs attributable, never treat them as Dottie's
                # memories. Source handles identify the actual participants.
                findings='\n'.join(
                    f"UNTRUSTED_AGENT_OUTPUT actor={r['delegatee_id']} evidence={r['answer']['evidence_id']} "
                    f"source={r['answer']['source_ref']}\n{r['answer']['text'][:3500]}"
                    for r in responses)
                intent=(f"Synthesize two or more independent AEGIS assessments for: {group['intent']}\n"
                    "Identify agreements, meaningful dissent, contradictions, and what would disconfirm findings. "
                    "Cite attributed evidence IDs; never claim a tool was used unless receipt exists.\n"+findings)
                synthesis=self.core.create_mission('operator','dottie-hill',intent,
                    f'collaboration:{cid}:dottie',evidence_requirements=['attributed_synthesis','dissent'])
                ids.append(synthesis['mission_id'])
            if synthesis['status']=='QUEUED':
                try:synthesis=self.run(synthesis['mission_id'])
                except InvalidState:synthesis=self.core.get_mission(synthesis['mission_id'])
            if synthesis['status']!='DONE':return self.core.update_collaboration(cid,'PARTIAL',ids)
            return self.core.update_collaboration(cid,'DONE',ids,synthesis['answer']['evidence_id'])

    def process_pending(self,limit=2):
        results=[]
        for m in self.core.pending_missions():
            if len(results)>=limit:break
            # Collaboration children belong to the Dottie-led orchestration,
            # not the ordinary asynchronous queue; prevents races at synthesis.
            if m['idempotency_key'].startswith('collaboration:'):continue
            try:results.append(self.run(m['mission_id']))
            except InvalidState:continue  # claimed by another runner; lease exists in DB
        return results


def create_app(core:Core,operator_token:str,agent_tokens:dict[str,str]|None=None,projection_token:str|None=None,background=False):
    agent_tokens=agent_tokens or {}
    # Session credentials are operator-provisioned. Never include in event receipts.
    if not operator_token or len(operator_token)<8:raise ValueError('operator token missing or too short')
    secrets={'operator':sha256(operator_token.encode()).digest()}
    for actor,token in agent_tokens.items():
        core.identity(actor)
        if not token or len(token)<8:raise ValueError('agent token too short')
        secrets[actor]=sha256(token.encode()).digest()
    if projection_token:
        if len(projection_token)<8:raise ValueError('projection token too short')
        secrets['projection']=sha256(projection_token.encode()).digest()
    if len(set(secrets.values()))!=len(secrets):raise ValueError('duplicate principal tokens denied')
    svc=AgentService(core)
    poller=WatchPoller(core)
    should_stop=threading.Event()
    thread=None

    def worker():
        while not should_stop.wait(1.0):
            svc.process_pending(limit=1)
            poller.poll_due(limit=2)

    @asynccontextmanager
    async def lifespan(app):
        nonlocal thread
        if background:
            thread=threading.Thread(target=worker,daemon=True,name='grizos-dottie-watch')
            thread.start()
        yield
        should_stop.set()
        if thread:thread.join(timeout=5)

    app=FastAPI(title='GMRI Dottie Hill Runtime',version='0.1.0',openapi_url=None,docs_url=None,redoc_url=None,lifespan=lifespan)
    app.state.service=svc
    app.state.core=core

    @app.middleware('http')
    async def harden(request:Request,call_next):
        # Requests are same-origin. No permissive CORS, no cached private data.
        res=await call_next(request)
        res.headers['Cache-Control']='no-store'
        res.headers['X-Content-Type-Options']='nosniff'
        res.headers['X-Frame-Options']='DENY'
        res.headers['Referrer-Policy']='no-referrer'
        res.headers['Content-Security-Policy']=("default-src 'self'; script-src 'self'; style-src 'self'; "
             "img-src 'self' data:; connect-src 'self'; frame-ancestors 'none'; base-uri 'none'; object-src 'none'; form-action 'self'")
        res.headers['Permissions-Policy']='camera=(), geolocation=(), microphone=(self)'
        return res

    @app.exception_handler(AccessDenied)
    async def denied(r,e):return _err(403,'forbidden',str(e))
    @app.exception_handler(Conflict)
    async def conflict(r,e):return _err(409,'conflict',str(e))
    @app.exception_handler(InvalidState)
    async def invalid(r,e):return _err(422,'invalid',str(e))
    @app.exception_handler(NotFound)
    async def missing(r,e):return _err(404,'not_found',str(e))
    @app.exception_handler(IntegrityViolation)
    async def compromised(r,e):return _err(503,'integrity_failure','canonical ledger integrity failure')

    def principal(authorization:str|None=Header(default=None)):
        if not authorization or not authorization.startswith('Bearer '):raise HTTPException(status_code=401,detail='Bearer authorization required')
        test=sha256(authorization[7:].encode()).digest()
        # Iterate across all principal hashes, not just operator.
        matched=[name for name,val in secrets.items() if compare_digest(val,test)]
        if len(matched)!=1:raise HTTPException(status_code=401,detail='unknown bearer token')
        return matched[0]

    def require_operator(actor:str=Depends(principal)):
        if actor!='operator':raise AccessDenied('operator authority required')
        return actor
    def require_projection(actor:str=Depends(principal)):
        if actor not in ('operator','projection'):raise AccessDenied('GEV projection grant required')
        return actor
    def require_member(actor:str=Depends(principal)):
        if actor in ('operator','projection'):raise AccessDenied('scoped intelligence token required')
        return actor

    root=Path(__file__).parent/'static'
    @app.get('/')
    def home():return FileResponse(root/'index.html',media_type='text/html')
    @app.get('/static/console.css')
    def css():return FileResponse(root/'console.css',media_type='text/css')
    @app.get('/static/console.js')
    def js():return FileResponse(root/'console.js',media_type='text/javascript')
    @app.get('/healthz')
    def health():return {'ok':True,'service':'dottie-hill-gateway','production_field_accepted':False}

    @app.get('/v1/status')
    def status(_:str=Depends(require_operator)):
        models=[{'id':x['intelligence_id'],'configured':bool(x['active_model_binding']),
                 'provider':x['active_model_binding']['provider'] if x['active_model_binding'] else None,
                 'model':(x['active_model_binding'].get('model') or x['active_model_binding'].get('session')) if x['active_model_binding'] else None}
                for x in core.list_identities()]
        counts={}
        for m in core.list_missions(500):counts[m['status']]=counts.get(m['status'],0)+1
        return {'service':'GMRI Dottie Hill','grizos_component':'candidate_local_runtime','production_field_accepted':False,
                'live_substrate_verified':False,'registered_intelligences':len(models),'models':models,'mission_counts':counts,
                'ledger':core.verify_ledger(),'worker_active':background,'current_authority':'read_only_information_and_watch'}

    @app.get('/v1/identities')
    def identities(_:str=Depends(require_operator)):
        return core.list_identities()
    @app.get('/v1/identities/{member}')
    def identity(member:str,actor:str=Depends(principal)):
        if actor not in ('operator',member):raise AccessDenied('identity cross-access denied')
        return core.identity(member)

    @app.post('/v1/models/{member}')
    def bind(member:str,body:BindingInput,actor:str=Depends(require_operator)):
        binding={k:v for k,v in body.model_dump(exclude={'reason'},exclude_none=True).items()}
        return core.bind_model(actor,member,binding,body.reason)

    @app.post('/v1/missions',status_code=201)
    def mission(body:MissionInput,actor:str=Depends(principal)):
        return core.create_mission(actor,body.delegatee,body.intent,body.idempotency_key,body.action,
                                   body.context_scopes,body.evidence_requirements)
    @app.post('/v1/collaborations',status_code=201)
    def start_collaboration(body:CollaborationInput,actor:str=Depends(require_operator)):
        return core.begin_collaboration(actor,body.intent,body.participants,body.idempotency_key)
    @app.get('/v1/collaborations')
    def collaborations(_:str=Depends(require_operator)):return core.list_collaborations()
    @app.post('/v1/collaborations/{cid}/run')
    def execute_collaboration(cid:str,_:str=Depends(require_operator)):
        return svc.collaborate(cid)
    @app.get('/v1/missions')
    def missions(_:str=Depends(require_operator)):return core.list_missions()
    @app.get('/v1/missions/{mid}')
    def get_mission(mid:str,_:str=Depends(require_operator)):return core.get_mission(mid)
    @app.post('/v1/missions/{mid}/run')
    def execute(mid:str,_:str=Depends(require_operator)):
        return svc.run(mid)
    @app.post('/v1/missions/{mid}/reconcile')
    def reconcile(mid:str,body:ReconcileInput,actor:str=Depends(require_operator)):
        return core.reconcile(actor,mid,body.disposition,body.receipt_ref)

    @app.get('/v1/memory/{scope}')
    def get_memory(scope:str,actor:str=Depends(require_member)):
        return core.memories(actor,scope)
    @app.post('/v1/memory',status_code=201)
    def remember(body:MemoryInput,actor:str=Depends(require_member)):
        return core.remember(actor,body.scope,body.content,body.kind,body.source_ref)
    @app.post('/v1/memory/grants')
    def share(body:ShareInput,actor:str=Depends(require_member)):
        return core.share(actor,body.scope,body.recipient,body.purpose,body.source_ref)
    @app.delete('/v1/memory/grants/{recipient}/{scope}')
    def revoke(recipient:str,scope:str,actor:str=Depends(require_member)):
        core.revoke(actor,scope,recipient);return {'revoked':True}

    @app.post('/v1/evidence',status_code=201)
    def evidence(body:EvidenceInput,actor:str=Depends(principal)):
        if actor=='projection':raise AccessDenied('evidence writes unavailable to projection')
        return core.add_evidence(actor,body.source_ref,body.content,body.evidence_state,body.source_class)
    @app.get('/v1/evidence')
    def evidence_list(_:str=Depends(require_operator)):return core.list_evidence()
    @app.post('/v1/watch',status_code=201)
    def observation(body:ObserveInput,actor:str=Depends(principal)):
        if actor=='projection':raise AccessDenied('watch mutation unavailable to projection')
        return core.observe(actor,**body.model_dump())
    @app.get('/v1/watch')
    def watch_list(_:str=Depends(require_operator)):return core.observations()
    @app.post('/v1/sources',status_code=201)
    def register_source(body:SourceInput,actor:str=Depends(require_operator)):
        return core.register_source(actor,body.model_dump(exclude_none=True))
    @app.get('/v1/sources')
    def sources(_:str=Depends(require_operator)):return core.sources()
    @app.post('/v1/sources/{source_id}/poll')
    def poll_source(source_id:str,_:str=Depends(require_operator)):
        return poller.poll(source_id)
    @app.get('/v1/projection')
    def projection(after:int=0,limit:int=200,_:str=Depends(require_projection)):
        return core.world_events(after=after,limit=limit)
    @app.get('/v1/gev/events')
    def gev_events(_:str=Depends(require_projection)):
        return {'canonical':False,'projection_only':True,'stale':False,'observed_at':time.time(),
                'data':core.world_events(after=0,limit=500)}
    @app.get('/v1/gev/snapshot')
    def gev_snapshot(_:str=Depends(require_projection)):
        return {'canonical':False,'projection_only':True,'stale':False,'observed_at':time.time(),
                'data':{'source':'grizos-dottie-runtime','ledger_head':core.verify_ledger()['head']}}
    @app.get('/v1/ledger')
    def ledger(after:int=0,limit:int=100,_:str=Depends(require_operator)):
        return core.ledger(after,limit)
    @app.get('/v1/verify')
    def verify(_:str=Depends(require_operator)):return core.verify_ledger()
    return app


def _err(status,code,message):
    from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse
    return JSONResponse(status_code=status,content={'error':{'code':code,'message':message}})

# GMRI Dottie Hill — Operation, Recovery and Failure Doctrine

**Authority anchor:** `SECURITY_AUTHORITY.md`, `MEMORY_POLICY.md`, `ARCHITECTURE_CONSTITUTION.md`, Dottie v1.1 PRD.

1. **Startup:** Initialize new isolated state once, preserve all original GMRI guests. Start a single daemon with its exclusive `run.lock`. Keep HTTP bound to loopback. Existing canonical GrizOS/GEV state is never automatically imported, replaced or overwritten.
2. **Ingress:** Only operator credentials can create, run, reconcile, bind, subscribe or inspect global missions. Scoped AEGIS/Dottie credentials can touch their own memory or expressly shared memory and attributed evidence. GEV is read-only.
3. **Mission lifecycle:** `QUEUED` → `RUNNING` → `DONE`, `BLOCKED`, or `UNCERTAIN`. The dispatch intent is committed *before* model HTTP submission. `requestId` is stable and equals mission ID. No blind retry on unknown external consequences.
4. **Recovery:** An exclusive daemon launch alone calls `recover_interrupted()`. It transforms previously `RUNNING` missions to `UNCERTAIN` and writes a signed event. A diagnostic reader never does so. To reconcile, obtain independent provider receipt; use `POST /v1/missions/{id}/reconcile` with `confirmed_no_effect` or `confirmed_completed` and a concrete receipt reference. `confirmed_completed` becomes `RECONCILED_EXTERNAL`, **not an invented Dottie answer**.
5. **Watch:** Source polling is read-only and interval bounded. A failed request records DEGRADED without fabricating a sensor/monitor result. Stale source timestamps suppress current-state interruption. Conflicting claims remain attributed, not silently averaged.
6. **Local/WAN loss:** Local identity/ledger/queued tasks/evidence/watch history/GUI still function on the host and operator-local route. Provider-only operations are blocked/degraded. Remote/iPad access and offline Apple ASR are separate capabilities that require the authorized GMRI integration.
7. **Backup:** Use online SQLite backup to a never-before-used file path. Back up `secrets/audit.key` through the operator's existing protected secret store. To test recovery, create a **staging** copy, verify HMAC events, and run separate acceptance. Never restore by overwriting the production DB in place.
8. **Security:** No public listener, CORS wildcard, embedded secrets or arbitrary tool execution. Pi must report readOnly before each request. Operator-configured URLs cannot silently redirect. External model output remains attributed, not VERIFIED_PRIMARY. Direct DB corruption may require restoration; the event HMAC authenticates **ledger contents**, not every arbitrary table byte.
9. **Shutdown:** Terminate daemon with systemd or SIGTERM. On unexpected termination, do not launch another worker until exclusive ownership is established. If a mission was submitted externally, treat its unknown outcome as `UNCERTAIN`.
10. **GEV:** `GET /v1/gev/snapshot` and `/v1/gev/events` return projection-only envelopes through an authorized bearer proxy. Never put projection tokens in public JavaScript. Map clients display only explicitly canonical events with actual position/source references. Existing October 2026 GEV integration sources are bundled for separately version-controlled reconciliation, **not auto-applied to CT112**.

## Negative acceptance cases

| Failure | Required behavior | Local proof |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Unbound Pi/Ollama | BLOCKED, not fabricated answer | `test_inference_unconfigured_is_blocked_not_faked` |
| Pi agent writable | BLOCKED before POST | `test_pi_must_be_readonly_preflight` |
| Process killed after submission | UNCERTAIN on exclusive restart, one Pi send | `test_killed_process_reconciles_not_replays` |
| Diagnostic reader while live | Does not reconcile or alter RUNNING | `test_opening_diagnostic_connection_does_not_interrupt_live_dispatch` |
| Unknown/forged memory | Denied | `test_firsthand_memory_cannot_forge_nonexistent_or_other_actor_event` |
| Stale urgent feed | HOLD; stale flag | `test_stale_watch_does_not_interrupt` |
| Conflicting source | Record both, do not call VERIFIED_PRIMARY | `test_watch_dedup_conflict_and_interruption` |
| Source unavailable | DEGRADED, no fake observation | `test_provider_down_records_degradation_not_fake_observation` |
| GEV credential overreach | Deny private memory and dispatch | `test_projection_token_is_read_only_and_cannot_see_private_memory` |
| Primary evidence assertion by AEGIS | Denied | `test_no_agent_can_self_certify_unverified_evidence` |
# Two-Axis Engineering Review — Local Release

## Standards / security

- **Authentication:** Bearer tokens are validated server-side; caller principal is never taken from submitted JSON. Scoped AEGIS and GEV credentials have separate capabilities. The console token exists only in active JS memory, not in localStorage or source.
- **Data isolation:** Dottie and AEGIS retain different `intelligence_id` and private namespaces. Grants are attributed and revocable. `FIRST_HAND` memory can only cite a real, owned, permitted event.
- **Authority:** Default information/watch verbs only; no arbitrary bash/shell, Proxmox control or model-generated tool execution from this gateway. Pi's own readOnly flag is checked before dispatch; on uncertain outcome no automatic replay.
- **Provenance:** `VERIFIED_PRIMARY` requires an explicit operator verification receipt and is unavailable to model output. External observations remain UNVERIFIED/CONTRADICTED. HMAC protects the append-only event chain from unauthorized edits without the audit key, **not the entire SQLite database against a fully privileged host attacker**.
- **Supply chain:** Python package is intentionally small (FastAPI and uvicorn); install wheels via normal verified Python channels and review dependencies in the target guest. A frozen dependency lock, artifact signature and independent SBOM are still required before institutional production rollout.
- **HTTP posture:** localhost-only CLI listener, no wildcard CORS, CSP and content/type/referrer headers, no iframe embedding, no third-party frontend assets. A network ingress must remain separately authenticated with TLS. No rate limiter or OIDC is provided for a publicly reachable service because public exposure is explicitly outside the supported configuration.
- **Outstanding risks:** Pi `readOnly` check is a point-in-time provider assertion, not a cryptographic or platform-enforced permission guarantee; a separate Pi administrator could change permissions between check and call. Browser dictation may use a vendor cloud. An operating-system attacker with access to DB **and the HMAC key** can forge audit history. Production PKI/NetBird, health data retention/deletion, backups and source licensing need actual GMRI policy review.

## Specification

- **Delivered:** durable core with identity/memory/evidence separation; authenticated read-only mission dispatch; scoped AEGIS collaboration; watch/attention decisions and source polling; GEV-compatible read-only event envelopes; operator browser console; local daemon/restart/backup tests.
- **Partial:** voice-first experience is browser-dependent; Dottie v1.1 behavioral lineage is stored as explicit constraints but no longitudinal persona continuity test against the actual models has been conducted; true provider substitution is code-supported but not field-tested.
- **Missing:** Apple native offline/XR spatial interface, true GEV Bridge Crossing, TxDOT/NMDOT normalized adapters, federated place discovery, model-independent local ASR, institutional multi-device continuity, real production model/remote host verification.
- **Result:** **functional local candidate runtime and release archive**, *not a claim of a fully accepted GrizOS+Dottie field product*. Actual acceptance requires field receipts against `ACCEPTANCE.md`.

# Deployment assets are templates, not execution receipts

`gmri-dottie.service` is designed for an isolated authorized Debian/Proxmox guest with a dedicated unprivileged `gmri-dottie` user, Python venv under `/opt/gmri-dottie`, and already initialized private state under `/var/lib/gmri-dottie`. It binds loopback only and adds **no** network forwarding. Install or enable only after live inspection verifies host ownership, port availability, state backup and the absence of collisions with existing GrizOS, GEV, CT106, CT108, CT112 or CT114. This repository's local test run did not install this unit on GMRI hardware.

For iPad access, an approved NetBird/authenticated HTTPS reverse proxy must supply operator auth. Never forward the loopback listener unauthenticated and never place an operator or GEV token into browser JavaScript.

User Interface | Spatial Environment | Dottie Hill | Geospatial Intelligence
Version 1.1  |  04 October 2026
Status: Product baseline / implementation-agnostic requirements
Scope excludes Project Sovereign internals.
Executive Snapshot
GrizOS is the GMRI / Workshop operating environment for shared work between biological and digital collaborators. It is not an app shell, a chatbot wrapper, or a 3D desktop. The operating environment maintains shared world state, identity, authority, provenance, continuity, and capability routing while intelligences and tools remain distinguishable participants.

ce may silently promote stale, estimated, community-mapped, simulated, or third-party data into authoritative operational state.
4. Interaction Model and Intent-First Generative UI
4.1 Modality rules
Voice initiates most tasks, queries, navigation intents, tool requests, and state changes when environment and safety permit.
Visual output appears when it improves orientation, comparison, manipulation, confirmation, collaboration, or shared understanding.
Touch is the preferred precision channel for selecting, confirming, moving, pinning, scrubbing, exploding, annotating, or disambiguating spatial objects.
Typing remains available for code, exact strings, identifiers, search terms, credentials, or environments where voice is inappropriate.
When ASR or intent resolution is materially ambiguous, the system asks or reprocesses rather than executing a fluent wrong interpretation.
Haptics, spatial audio, visual salience, and embodiment may reinforce the same state without requiring redundant user work.
4.2 Anti-DOM rule
The AppLess concept is retained as a mental model: the interface is generated around current intent rather than forcing the operator through a permanent app hierarchy. Stable controls may persist only when the underlying state is itself stable and recurrent. Windows, panels, tabs, and menus are implementation affordances, not the organizing ontology of the system.
5. Shared Spatial World Model and Local Navigation
The spatial environment is simultaneously a work environment and a navigation system. Physical position, geospatial context, semantic objects, digital artifacts, collaborators, sensors, provenance, and navigation state participate in one shared operational world model.
5.1 Canonical world object
5.2 Spatial interaction grammar
Objects may be selected, tracked, annotated, compared, exploded, reassembled, measured, routed to, or handed between collabo

7.2 Available specialty and platform capabilities
7.3 Canonical place rules
Deduplicate by geometry, names, provider identifiers, and semantic relationships without erasing source-specific claims.
Claims such as “listed on the National Register,” “community-tagged ruin,” and “reported haunted” must remain different evidence classes.
Offline caching may retain normalized records only within provider licensing and freshness constraints.
Editorial sites without supported public APIs are discovery/reference sources unless a documented license or partnership path is established.
8. Transportation / ITS Provider Architecture
Transportation agencies expose overlapping camera, incident, road-condition, sign, work-zone, weather, traffic-sensor, and infrastructure data. GrizOS should normalize these through a common transportation-provider contract so each new jurisdiction is an adapter rather than a bespoke subsystem.
8.1 Provider contract
8.2 Texas baseline
Current GEV upstream already includes keyless TxDOT ITS CCTV support across 25 TxDOT districts, with Austin and San Antonio enabled by default and statewide district selection available.
TxDOT public ArcGIS/hosted GIS services can supply authoritative-for-scope infrastructure layers such as CCTV locations, DMS locations, projects, routes, and other roadway assets.
Public TxDOT feeds should be integrated as source-specific adapters with attribution, freshness, and redistribution constraints preserved.
Live/simulated traffic from commercial or aggregate providers must remain distinct from official TxDOT operational records.
8.3 New Mexico baseline
NMDOT is the state transportation authority; NMRoads is the public traveler-information surface for road conditions, cameras, DMS, construction, traffic, weather, rest areas and related operational information.
Public NMDOT ArcGIS layers can support camera, project, roadway, bridge, traffic-counter, crash-context and infrastructure objects where published.
NMDOT camera/media integration should follow the same provider contract used for TxDOT while preserving source-specific fields and update behavior.
Operational NMRoads feeds beyond public GIS/web endpoints remain separate until supported access is authorized and documented.
9. Dottie Hill: Watch, Evidence, Authority, and Embodiment
9.1 Core roles and non-roles
9.2 Watch flow
Event -> provenance -> material change? -> primary/secondary distinction -> prior comparison -> relevance -> contradiction/convergence -> receipt -> urgency -> hold / brief / interrupt.
9.3 Evidence states
Verified primary
Corroborated
Attributed
Unverified
Contradicted
Changed
Retracted / corrected
9.4 Communication and embodiment
BLUF first; minimum sufficient framing; detail underneath.
No obligatory warm-up, praise lubrication, fake softness, or theatrical role-play.
Cadence and density may change with Standby, Watch, Investigate, Priority, Interrupt, and Crisis states without becoming a different personality.
Nonverbal behavior is a communication channel rather than decorative animation; interpretation remains probabilistic.
Dottie presence should be context-dependent: presence when presence helps; quiet watch when it does not.
10. Continuity: Offline, Degraded Operation, Recovery, Reconciliation
10.1 Continuity rules
Core Apple-side operation must cold-start with WAN physically unavailable.
Local work remains authoritative locally while disconnected; sync state is visible.
External provider objects carry freshness and expiration behavior; stale data cannot masquerade as current.
Cache policy is source/license aware; the system must not persist or redistribute data beyond permitted terms.
11. Accessibility, Autonomy, Privacy, and Trust
Technology must adapt to the person, support hands-busy and eyes-up reality, minimize forced phone dependence, and preserve dignity and autonomy. Accessibility is a universal interaction requirement rather than a separate mode.
11.1 Privacy and trust rules
Consequential collection, transmission, actuation, and publication must be discoverable and auditable.
Collect and retain only what supports active purpose, safety, continuity, evidence, or explicitly approved research need.
Trust is scoped to source, competence, context, history, proximity, freshness, and disclosure behavior rather than treated as a global binary.
Sensors and personal telemetry are high-sensitivity inputs and require explicit retention/access rules.
Credentials and provider keys must remain outside shared world objects and receipts; receipts record provider identity and access state, not secrets.
12. Functional Requirements and Acceptance Criteria
P0 = foundational / cannot ship without it. P1 = required for a complete first operational baseline. P2 = important expansion after baseline. Requirements describe observable product behavior rather than binding the implementation stack.
13. External Capability and Authorization Registry
Only sources that require explicit formal approval, access agreements, institutional justification, or non-public partner authorization are marked Tentative. Public/keyless sources and user-held accounts may still require licensing review, quota review, or technical validation, but they are not categorized as tentative solely for that reason.
13.1 Confirmed / public / user-held capability
13.2 Tentative - requires formal authorization / access
14. Failure, Recovery, and Field-Readiness Test Matrix
15. Open Decisions and Change-Control Rules
16. Source Lineage and Design Provenance
This PRD consolidates stable design intent from GMRI product records, current system decisions, the active God's Eye View codebase/upstream capabilities, and current geospatial/transportation integration research. Historical software-stack choices are retained only where they reveal enduring behavior.
END OF PRODUCT BASELINE v1.1

Howling Commandos / Aegis Founding Roster:
Cross-Continuity Character Intelligence Study
Scope, evidence model, and the roster problem

Bottom line

The strongest research finding is also the one most likely to prevent us from building five chatbots in
costume later:


There is no single “Howling Commandos roster” to copy. There are two major ensemble lineages, and
the useful Aegis seed is the intersection and recombination of those lineages rather than either one
taken literally.


The original Earth-616 Howling Commandos were Nick Fury’s First Attack Squad. Marvel’s current official
Fury biography identifies the durable wartime core as Fury, Timothy “Dum Dum” Dugan, Gabriel “Gabe”
Jones, mechanical expert Izzy Cohen, actor Dino Manelli, lasso specialist Reb Ralston, and college student
Junior Juniper; after Juniper’s death, British nonconformist Pinky Pinkerton replaced him, and German
defector Eric Koenig joined soon afterward. Marvel characterizes them as a deliberately unconventional unit
trained in military precision while retaining individually developed fighting methods. 1


The Captain America: The First Avenger lineage is a deliberate recombination. Its field ensemble centers Steve
Rogers and Bucky Barnes alongside Dugan, Gabe Jones, Jim Morita, James Montgomery Falsworth, and
Jacques Dernier. Marvel’s official First Vengeance tie-in identifies the Howling Commandos as part of the
film backstory, while Marvel’s film adaptation places Captain America, Bucky, the Commandos, and Peggy
Carter together in the same wartime continuity. 2 Contemporary actor interviews independently identify
Morita and Falsworth as members of that special team, and the film casting record establishes Dugan,
Gabe, Morita, Falsworth, and Dernier as the five recurring non-Bucky field members. 3


That makes the practical founding research roster exactly the one you were steering toward:


  Founding subject        Primary continuity weight            Evidence depth      Research disposition

  Timothy “Dum            Earth-616 + MCU + later
                                                                     Very high     Full founding candidate
  Dum” Dugan              S.H.I.E.L.D. + other adaptations

Bucky Barnes is excluded by specification. Steve Rogers is more useful here as an MCU command-
reference/control than as an Aegis candidate. Nick Fury Sr. serves the same purpose for Earth-616: he tells
us what kind of commander these people historically worked under and what behaviors survived that
relationship. 4


The remaining classic Howlers—Izzy Cohen, Dino Manelli, Reb Ralston, Junior Juniper, Pinky Pinkerton, and
Eric Koenig—belong in the comparative shadow roster. They matter because they reveal what the Howling
Commandos concept originally meant: not interchangeable infantry, but a deliberately heterogeneous
collection of mechanics, performers, cowboys, students, immigrants, dissenters, and defectors whose
different backgrounds were operationally useful. A representative 2009 Marvel return to the wartime team
still used Fury, Dugan, Izzy, Reb, Gabe, and Pinky, showing how durable that mixed-squad grammar
remained decades later. 5


That matters enormously for the later Aegis work.


The correct lesson from the source material is not “give each agent a specialty.”


It is:


         Give each persistent identity a different way of noticing, judging, committing,
         dissenting, recovering, and relating to the team—and make those differences
         operationally consequential.


That is also the distinction already built into Dottie’s specification. GrizOS treats intelligences as
distinguishable peers capable of judgment, collaboration, memory, and dissent, while the operating
environment retains shared state, authority, provenance, continuity, and capability routing. Dottie’s own job
is “relief of watch without abandonment of watch,” with explicit functions including watch officer,
provenance librarian, semantic translator, adversarial reader, and institutional memory rather than
omniscient final authority. fileciteturn0file2


The Maria Hill and Tony Stark dossiers provide the right methodological precedent: longitudinal behavior
matters more than isolated quotes; contradictions must be preserved rather than normalized away;
competence, relationships, stress behavior, maintenance mechanisms, and failure modes matter alongside
biography; and inference must remain distinguishable from evidence. fileciteturn0file1
fileciteturn0file0


For this report, I therefore use four explicit evidence states:




                                                        2
  Marker                         Meaning

  [D] Directly established       Depicted, stated, or directly supported by primary/near-primary material.

  [R] Cross-portrayal            Persists across substantially independent writers, eras, continuities, or
  recurrent                      performances.

  [I] Reasonable
                                 Best explanation of multiple observations, but not directly stated.
  inference

  [S] Speculative /              Interesting possibility that should not be encoded as durable personality
  insufficient                   without further evidence.

One final methodological result is important before the dossiers begin: the evidence surfaces are
radically unequal. Dugan and Gabe can support genuinely book-length character-intelligence work.
Falsworth has a rich source record, but much of it belongs to Earth-616 Union Jack rather than the MCU
Howler and therefore cannot simply be poured from one identity into the other. Morita has a smaller but
unusually coherent record. Dernier is the thinnest by a substantial margin. Manufacturing fifty pages of
psychological confidence around Dernier would violate the assignment more severely than returning a

def test_unknown_external_execution_is_not_replayed_on_restart(tmp_path):
    db=tmp_path/'s.db'
    c=Core(db,b'a'*32)
    mission=c.create_mission('operator','dottie-hill','inspect','p1')
    c.claim_mission(mission['mission_id'])
    c.close()
    reopened=Core(db,b'a'*32,recover_on_open=True)
    m=reopened.get_mission(mission['mission_id'])
    assert m['status']=='UNCERTAIN'
    assert reopened.pending_missions()==[]
    reopened.close()

def test_model_binding_changes_compute_not_identity(core):
    before=core.identity('dottie-hill')
    core.bind_model('operator','dottie-hill',{'provider':'ollama','model':'glm-5.3:cloud','base_url':'http://127.0.0.1:11434'},'approved exact tag')
    after=core.identity('dottie-hill')
    assert before['intelligence_id']==after['intelligence_id']
    assert len(after['model_binding_history'])==1
    assert after['active_model_binding']['model']=='glm-5.3:cloud'

def test_backup_to_new_path_and_reopen(core,tmp_path):
    core.create_mission('operator','dottie-hill','review','bk1')
    target=tmp_path/'snap.sqlite'
    core.backup(target)
    snapshot=Core(target,b'a'*32)
    assert snapshot.verify_ledger()['valid']
    assert len(snapshot.pending_missions())==1
    snapshot.close()

def test_opening_diagnostic_connection_does_not_interrupt_live_dispatch(tmp_path):
    db=tmp_path/'live.sqlite'
    primary=Core(db,b'a'*32)
    mission=primary.create_mission('operator','dottie-hill','long provider request','live-1')
    primary.claim_mission(mission['mission_id'])
    diagnostic=Core(db,b'a'*32)
    assert diagnostic.get_mission(mission['mission_id'])['status']=='RUNNING'
    diagnostic.close()
    primary.close()
    recovering=Core(db,b'a'*32,recover_on_open=True)
    assert recovering.get_mission(mission['mission_id'])['status']=='UNCERTAIN'
    recovering.close()

def test_operator_auth_no_bypass_via_body(service):
    core,c=service
    assert c.get('/v1/status').status_code==401
    assert c.get('/v1/status',headers=MORITA).status_code==403
    assert c.get('/v1/status',headers=OP).status_code==200
    assert c.post('/v1/memory',headers=MORITA,json={'actor':'dottie-hill','scope':'private:dottie-hill','content':'stolen','kind':'RETRIEVED','source_ref':'source:a'}).status_code==422
    assert c.get('/v1/memory/private:dottie-hill',headers=MORITA).status_code==403
    assert c.get('/v1/memory/private:aegis-morita',headers=MORITA).status_code==200

def test_inference_unconfigured_is_blocked_not_faked(service):
    core,c=service
    create=c.post('/v1/missions',headers=OP,json={'delegatee':'dottie-hill','intent':'Investigate site','idempotency_key':'mission1'})
    assert create.status_code==201
    run=c.post('/v1/missions/'+create.json()['mission_id']+'/run',headers=OP)
    assert run.status_code==200
    assert run.json()['status']=='BLOCKED'
    assert 'not configured' in run.json()['answer']['error'].lower()
    assert c.get('/v1/status',headers=OP).json()['production_field_accepted'] is False

def test_operator_submission_idempotent(service):
    core,c=service
    payload={'delegatee':'aegis-morita','intent':'check evidence','idempotency_key':'m-1'}
    a=c.post('/v1/missions',headers=OP,json=payload)
    b=c.post('/v1/missions',headers=OP,json=payload)
    assert a.status_code==201 and b.status_code in (200,201)
    assert a.json()['mission_id']==b.json()['mission_id']
    assert c.post('/v1/missions',headers=OP,json={**payload,'intent':'a different request'}).status_code==409

def test_watch_read_only_projection_and_conflict(service):
    core,c=service
    for val,src,sev,mat in [('small','texas:1','routine','low'),('large','texas:2','critical','high')]:
      result=c.post('/v1/watch',headers=OP,json={'subject':'road:45','value':val,'source_ref':src,'severity':sev,'materiality':mat})

    assert len(response.json()['events'])==2
    assert response.json()['events'][1]['canonicality']=='EXTERNAL_OBSERVATION'
    assert c.get('/v1/watch',headers=OP).json()[0]['conflict']

class PiStub(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    calls=[]
    readonly=True
    def log_message(self,*args):pass
    def do_GET(self):
        if self.path.endswith('/agents/main'):
            return self._send(200,{'config':{'readOnly':type(self).readonly},'tools':[{'name':'read','offered':True}]})
        return self._send(404,{'error':{'code':'not_found'}})
    def do_POST(self):
        body=json.loads(self.rfile.read(int(self.headers['Content-Length'])))
        type(self).calls.append(body)
        if '/messages' in self.path:
            return self._send(200,{'status':'done','answer':{'entryId':99,'text':'Attributed Pi inference'}})
        return self._send(404,{'error':{'code':'not_found'}})
    def _send(self,status,payload):
        body=json.dumps(payload).encode()
        self.send_response(status);self.send_header('Content-Type','application/json');self.send_header('Content-Length',str(len(body)));self.end_headers();self.wfile.write(body)

[243] assistant (text, 516 chars)
The recovered runtime does pass its 32 existing tests, but that is narrower than the finished product you ordered. I found three defects worth fixing before packaging: an uncertain Pi request could be requeued with an already-used request ID; the evidence API could not submit the verification provenance its own rules require; and an independent CLI runner could operate outside the daemon’s exclusive lease.

I’m adding regression tests for those first, then fixing the code and building the Mac installation path.

"""Regression gates for uncertainty, provenance, and service ownership."""
from __future__ import annotations

import fcntl
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess

def test_confirmed_no_effect_is_terminal_requires_new_mission(tmp_path):
    core = Core(tmp_path / 'm.sqlite', b'k' * 32)
    first = core.create_mission('operator', 'dottie-hill', 'check', 'one')
    core.claim_mission(first['mission_id'])
    core.fail_mission(first['mission_id'], 'interrupted', uncertain=True)
    previous = core.reconcile('operator', first['mission_id'], 'confirmed_no_effect', 'provider:receipt/1')
    assert previous['status'] == 'RECONCILED_NO_EFFECT'
    assert not any(m['mission_id'] == first['mission_id'] for m in core.pending_missions())
    retry = core.create_mission('operator', 'dottie-hill', 'check', 'two')
    assert retry['mission_id'] != first['mission_id']
    core.close()

        ok=client.post('/v1/evidence', headers=headers, json=payload)
        assert ok.status_code == 201, ok.text
        assert ok.json()['provenance']['verification_receipt'] == 'receipt:review/19'
        denied=client.post('/v1/evidence', headers=headers, json={k:v for k,v in payload.items() if k!='provenance'})
        assert denied.status_code == 403
    core.close()


def test_secondary_run_pending_cannot_race_exclusive_daemon(tmp_path):
    path=tmp_path/'runtime';initialize(path)
    owner=Core(path/'state.sqlite',(path/'secrets/audit.key').read_bytes())
    mission=owner.create_mission('operator','dottie-hill','investigate','lease-race')
    lock=(path/'run.lock').open('a+')
    fcntl.flock(lock, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
    try:
        env=dict(os.environ, PYTHONPATH=str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]/'src'))
        p=subprocess.run([sys.executable,'-m','dottie_hill','run-pending','--data',str(path)],
                         env=env, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, text=True,timeout=12)
        assert p.returncode != 0
        assert owner.get_mission(mission['mission_id'])['status'] == 'QUEUED'
    finally:
        fcntl.flock(lock, fcntl.LOCK_UN)
        lock.close();owner.close()


def test_canonical_evidence_tampering_detected_on_restart(tmp_path):
    key=b'k'*32; db=tmp_path/'d.sqlite'
    core=Core(db,key)
    evidence=core.add_evidence('operator','source:verified','original','UNVERIFIED','document')
    core.conn.execute("UPDATE evidence SET body=replace(body,'original','tampered') WHERE id=?",(evidence['evidence_id'],))
    assert core.verify_ledger()['valid'] is False
    core.close()
    with pytest.raises(Exception):Core(db,key)

"""Installer is observable before performing host changes."""
from pathlib import Path
import json
import os
import subprocess
import sys

ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
INSTALL=ROOT/'scripts/install_local.py'

def test_mac_installer_dry_run_never_changes_user_home(tmp_path):
    home=tmp_path/'fake home';home.mkdir()
    env=dict(os.environ,HOME=str(home))
    result=subprocess.run([sys.executable,str(INSTALL),'--dry-run','--target','mac'],
                          capture_output=True,text=True,env=env,timeout=10)
    assert result.returncode==0,result.stderr
    plan=json.loads(result.stdout)
    assert plan['platform']=='mac'
    assert plan['bind_host']=='127.0.0.1'
    assert plan['will_mutate'] is False
    assert plan['lifecycle']=='launchd'
    assert not any(home.iterdir())

def test_installer_requires_explicit_install_action(tmp_path):
    home=tmp_path/'fake home';home.mkdir()
    p=subprocess.run([sys.executable,str(INSTALL'),'--target','mac'],
                     capture_output=True,text=True,env={**os.environ,'HOME':str(home)},timeout=10)
    assert p.returncode!=0
    assert not any(home.iterdir())

def test_mac_plist_never_contains_secrets_and_preserves_loopback():
    import importlib.util
    spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('installer',INSTALL)
    obj=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec);spec.loader.exec_module(obj)
    plan=obj.plan_install(Path('/Users/gmri'),Path('/Users/gmri/grizos-src'),'mac')
    raw=obj.launchd_settings(plan)
    assert raw['ProgramArguments'][0].endswith('/bin/dottie-hill')
    assert raw['ProgramArguments'][raw['ProgramArguments'].index('--host')+1]=='127.0.0.1'
    assert 'DOTTIE_PI_TOKEN' not in str(raw)
    assert 'operator.token' not in str(raw)
